「チャイナショック2.0」に揺れる世界
〜中国の「強すぎる供給⼒」がもたらす構造変化〜
【みずほ経済インサイト】

### Page 2

本資料の要旨
1
１．「チャイナショック2.0」とは何か P2～12
１．「チャイナショック2.0」とは何か P2～12
• 「チャイナショック」とは、中国の「強すぎる供給力」と輸出攻勢が世界の産業・貿易構造を変える現象
• 「2.0」の特徴は、労働集約型産業での競争力を維持したまま、技術・資本集約型産業での競争力を高めていること
• 背景にあるのが中国特有の技術革新モデル。産業集積と規模の経済もあり、中国企業の競争力が格段に向上
２．「チャイナショック2.0」に揺れる欧州とASEAN P13～24
２．「チャイナショック2.0」に揺れる欧州とASEAN P13～24
• 欧州は輸出減少と輸入増加に直面。基幹産業でも中国製品の存在感が高まり、自動車産業を中心に競争が本格化
• ASEANは中国からの中間財調達が輸出産業にとって必須化。ただし、最終財を中心に域内産品との競合は強まる
• 中国製品の浸透を受け、欧州、ASEANとも貿易障壁措置を拡大。中国企業は貿易障壁を意識し、現地生産を志向

３．「チャイナショック2.0」に相対する世界 P25～32
３．「チャイナショック2.0」に相対する世界 P25～32
• 世界は競争と相互依存のジレンマに直面。国内製造業の強化で中国に対抗しつつ、安価な中国製品の輸入も継続
• 中国は、対中規制や追加関税への報復措置をちらつかせつつ、自由貿易体制の維持を図って自国の利益を最大化
• 中国が新興国においてAI経済圏を形成し、世界経済システムを再構築する「チャイナショック3.0」に発展する可能性も
（出所） みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

2005年 2015年 2024年（％）
「チャイナショック2.0」とは何か――中国の「強すぎる供給力」が世界を変える現象
 中国の「強すぎる供給力」と輸出攻勢が世界の産業・貿易構造を変える「チャイナショック2.0」が進行中
― 中国がWTO加盟を契機に生産・組立拠点として世界貿易体制に組み込まれたのが「チャイナショック1.0」
―「2.0」では、中国企業の競争力と供給力が飛躍的に向上し、先端産業分野で先進国との競合が激化
 「チャイナショック2.0」の特徴は、中国が労働集約型産業での輸出競争力を維持したまま、技術・資本集約型産業での競争
力を高めていること。中国企業は繊維・履物などの軽工業品からハイテク製品まで幅広い分野で輸出を伸ばしている
― 他方、「自立自強」政策による輸入代替の進展と内需の低迷で輸入が停滞。その結果、貿易不均衡が拡大
3
（出所） みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

「チャイナショック2.0」はなぜ起きたのか――「中国式イノベーション」と製造業の生産性向上
 「チャイナショック2.0」の背景にあるのが「中国式イノベーション」。新興分野で中国の民営企業が躍進
― 「中国式イノベーション」とは、国家の戦略的主導と民間の市場志向が組み合わさった技術革新モデル（筆者定義）
― その土台にあるのが産業集積と規模の経済。こうした環境下、中国企業の技術力とコスト競争力が格段に向上
 中国の製造業は、政府の産業支援も背景に積極的な設備投資を行い、自動化と生産の効率化が急速に進展
― 産業用ロボットの新規導入台数（2025年）は35.4万台と世界（60.3万台）の過半を占め、稼働台数でも群を抜く
― 世界経済フォーラム（WEF）が認定するライトハウス（先進工場）拠点数で中国は最多。特に生産性の評価が高い

4
「中国式イノベーション」の特徴（筆者定義） 産業用ロボット稼働台数（左）とライトハウス拠点数（右）

製造業付加価値は世界の3割弱を占める規模。貿易構造が高度化しつつある様子も
 中国はすでに「世界の工場」の地位を確立。製造業付加価値（2025年）は4.8兆ドルと、世界の3割弱を占める規模
― 中国の輸出額（2025年）は3.77兆ドルと、世界の14.4％を占める。貿易黒字も1.18兆ドルと圧倒的な大きさ
― 輸出総額だけでなく、技術集約的なハイテク製品（航空、コンピューターなど）の輸出額も8,250億ドルと世界一
 加えて、近年は貿易構造が消費財（最終財）中心から中間財や資本財へと高度化しつつある様子もうかがえる
― より付加価値の高い製品の貿易特化係数（輸出入バランスから貿易品目の国際競争力を示す指標）が上昇傾向
5

軽工業品の高い競争力を維持しつつ、技術力が必要な化学や機械でも競争力向上

 中国は、軽工業品の高い競争力を維持しつつ、技術力が必要な化学や機械などでも競争力を向上させている
― HSコード別の貿易特化係数を見ると、2025年は2005年と比較して輸出競争力が大幅に向上している品目が多い
― 他方で、食品・原材料・素材の多くは輸入に頼る。「世界の工場」における需要拡大や食の多様化などが背景に
6

中国と先進国との輸出競合度が急上昇。自動車が最大の上昇要因
 中国と先進国の輸出類似度指数（ESI）は「チャイナショック2.0」で急上昇。両者の競合が強まっていることを示唆
― 日本は「1.0」と「2.0」の時期に、EUは2010年代中盤から「2.0」の時期にかけて、ESIが顕著に上昇
― 実際、EUは「2.0」の「震度」が大きく、中国製品の浸透による製造業の衰退が最も懸念されている地域
― 産業別に見ると、「2.0」では日本、EUともに自動車が最大の上昇要因になっていることが分かる
10
中国と先進国の輸出類似度指数（ESI） ESI上昇の産業別寄与度（コロナ禍前、コロナ禍後）

（注） SITC品目別3ケタ分類より算出
（出所） UN Comtradeより、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

①チャイナショック2.0とは何か

### Page 12

「チャイナショック2.0」のもう一つの特徴は輸入の停滞。背景に「自立自強」の進展
 「チャイナショック2.0」のもう一つの特徴は輸入の停滞。「自立自強」による輸入代替（国産化）の進展が背景に
― かつて加工貿易が主流であった頃は、輸出製品を生産するため中間財や資本財の輸入が増え、先進国にも恩恵
― 世界の輸出入に占める中国のシェアを見ると、輸出が「1.0」の頃からほぼ一貫して上昇しているのに対し、輸入は2010
年代に入って頭打ちとなっていることが分かる
― 世界の輸入に占める中国のシェアを品目別に見ても、2024年は多くの業種で2015年からシェアが縮小傾向にある

### Page 22

ASEAN5：国産品・中国産品の調達額変化の比較
ASEAN：一方、中国産品とASEAN域内産品の競合は強まる傾向
21
 素材や消費財、機械・電気機器等では、中国製品がASEAN域内産品を圧迫。最終需要で競合する分野が多い傾向
― 上記分野では中国産品の供給増と域内産品の供給減が同時に発生。中国産品が域内産品を上回って増加する例も
― 中間需要は3分野が該当。一方、最終需要では5分野が該当し、消費財等の部門でより強く競合している可能性を示唆

22
 EUは中国製品の流入拡大を背景に、貿易措置を積極化
― 対中貿易措置は2023年以降に増加。化学品や鉄鋼など幅広い品目が対象。2024年には中国製EVへの追加関税を発

 ASEANも貿易保護措置を拡大する傾向。鉄鋼製品等へのアンチダンピング・セーフガードのほか、越境ECへも対応を拡大
― ただし、対中貿易措置の比率は目立って高まらず。外交関係への配慮から、中国以外の地域も含む形で規制を強化

「チャイナショック2.0」の光と影――安価な製品供給の恩恵と先進国の競争力喪失リスク
 中国の「強すぎる供給力」は、世界市場に低価格と安定供給という恩恵をもたらしている
― 中国の輸出価格が相対的に低下する一方、輸出数量はハイペースで拡大しており、輸出ドライブが鮮明に
― 輸出ドライブによる安価な中国製品の供給拡大が世界の財価格を押し下げ、インフレ圧力の緩和にも寄与
 他方で、中国の高い輸出競争力は輸入国の産業競争力の低下と対中依存度の上昇を招いていると考えられる

― 先進国が中国企業の技術革新力とコスト競争力に圧倒され、得意としてきた先端産業で競争力を喪失するリスク
― 関税などで対中輸入を抑制しても、供給網を通じた中国依存は容易に低下せず。経済安保上の脆弱性は残存
26

### Page 28

「チャイナショック2.0」への対応――国内製造業の強化で対抗しつつ、中国製品の輸入も継続
 世界は競争と相互依存のジレンマに直面。国内製造業の強化で中国に対抗しつつ、安価な中国製品の輸入も継続
― 先進国は、戦略分野での産業基盤の強化や対中デリスキングを図りつつ、非機微分野で貿易・投資活動を維持
― 新興国は、脱中国化を図る供給網の一部を取り込んで国内産業の育成を企図。過剰な中国製品の流入には警戒も
 中国は、対中規制や追加関税への報復措置をちらつかせつつ、自由貿易体制の維持を図って自国の利益を最大化
― 過剰生産能力への批判を一蹴し輸出拡大。一方で、関税回避や規制対応のため、海外での現地生産も積極展開
27
「チャイナショック2.0」への対抗と協調
（出所） みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

### Page 29

EUは産業競争力の回復に向け、政策ロードマップを策定。IAAにより域内調達も強化へ
 第二期フォンデアライエン体制下で、EUは「競争力回復」に向けた政策のロードマップ（競争力コンパス）を策定
― （１）米中とのイノベーション格差是正、（２）脱炭素化と競争力強化の両立、（３）過剰な域外依存の軽減と安全保障の強
化を目指す。加えて、市場統合の深化や規制緩和等を内容とする（４）分野横断的取り組みを実行する構え
 競争力コンパス枠組みのもと、欧州は原産地規則の強化により、EVや鉄鋼産業等の域内調達を強力に推進する方針
― 2026年3月以降、欧州委員会は域内調達の拡大に向け、IAA（産業加速法）の立法化に着手
― IAAの現行案では、EVの公共調達や補助金等の支援に対し、域内調達・組立の要件を導入予定
（注） 7要件：①実質的な内国民待遇、②経済安全保障、③競争条件＋ILO、④パリ協定＋炭素価格、⑤企業行動・人権、⑥低炭素需要政策、⑦迂回防止
（出所） 欧州委員会（2025）“A Competitiveness Compass for the EU(factsheet)”、ほか各種資料より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

欧州企業の行動にも変化の兆し。AIや再エネなど重点分野への投資が拡大

### Page 31

政策の概要国
• 2026年9月、輸入電気自動車への物品税引上げを決定。バッテリーEVのうち国内生産がなく、国産部品も使
用していない車両には2027年から物品税を10％→35％に引き上げる計画
• 現地部品の使用など、一定の国内調達や今後の調達率引き上げが見込まれるものについては、税率引き下
げも検討
タイ
• 四輪電気自動車（BEV）について国産化率規定を設置。政府は2026年までに40％、2030年以降に80％と段
階的な基準値引き上げを目指す
• 完成車輸入優遇の利用企業に、2027年末までに輸入台数以上の国内生産を義務付け
インドネシア
• 2026年7月、EVの国内生産・部品製造を支援するEVインセンティブ戦略（EVIS）を導入。交付された納税証
明書を各種納税に充当することで、企業が税務負担を軽減できる仕組み
• 一定の投資・生産要件を満たす企業に対し、投資額の最大40％を支給
• 国内製造・組立車両の生産実績に応じ、工場出荷価格の最大12％相当を支給
フィリピン
• 国内組立EVや関連部品の税金を2027年末まで一部免除（EV本体：物品税・販売税、部品：輸入関税）

• 完成車輸入に車両価格、およびエンジン容量の下限を設定（2026年7月～）マレーシア
• 国内で製造・組立を行い、生産台数等の要件を満たす企業に、国内生産できない自動車部品の輸入関税を
免除。環境対応車も対象ベトナム
ASEANは、対中関係深化のメリットを維持すべく、自動車を中心に現地生産を促す
30
 ASEANは現地生産要件の設置などで、中国との貿易関係や中国企業の投資流入を維持しながら、輸入品との競合による
域内産業への圧力緩和を目指す方向
― 2025年以降、ASEAN5各国は、自動車を中心に現地生産の促進や国内規定を徐々に厳格化
― 輸入車を中心にEVの浸透が進む中、現地生産化の促進により、中国企業による域内投資や地場サプライヤー・雇用へ
の波及効果を得る狙い

### Page 32

おわりに：「チャイナショック2.0」とAI――製造業へのAI実装で一段と高まる中国の競争力
 中国は、AI技術で世界をリード。オープンウェイト戦略と国家主導のガバナンスで独自のAIエコシステムを構築しつつある
― 大規模言語モデルの性能は米国に肉薄。コストパフォーマンスで海外にも普及し、トークン消費量のシェアが急拡大
― 同時に、ヒューマノイドや自動運転といったフィジカルAI分野でも、技術開発と社会実装が急ピッチで進んでいる
 中国は今後、製造業へのAI実装によって生産性を向上、技術革新を加速させ、競争力を一段と高めるとみられる
― 中国政府は、経済社会の幅広い分野でAI導入を図る「AI＋（プラス）」政策を推進。「AI＋製造業」により研究開発設計、
生産、物流、サービスなど各バリューチェーンへの実装が進めば、「強すぎる供給力」がさらに強力に

 「チャイナショック3.0」は、中国がグローバルサウスにおいてAI経済圏を形成し、世界経済システムを再構築する現象
― 中国は独自の「AIエコシステム」の海外展開でAI経済圏を形成し、既存の技術秩序と産業・貿易構造を変容させる
― また、「世界AI協力機構（WAICO）」などを通じて国際協調を図り、AI経済に対応した標準・制度作りを主導する
― AIを軸とした「3.0」では、モノに留まらない形で中国の存在感が強まる可能性。世界にさらなる変化への対応を迫りうる

市場焦點
美國
• 9月ISM服務業PMI降至54.9，雖商業活動與新訂單放緩，但就業回升至50.1、訂單積壓增加，
顯示服務需求仍具韌性；另一方面，價格指數升至74.0，創2022年7月以來新高，供應商交貨
速度亦放緩，反映燃料、原物料及供應鏈成本升高，景氣未明顯轉弱之際，通膨壓力反而重
新升溫；同時全球長債殖利率同步走高，AI基建投資延續企業成長動能，也提高資本需求。
整體而言，美國經濟尚未明顯轉弱，但通膨與長端利率壓力同步升高，使金融條件維持偏緊。
中國
• 彭博指出在政府積極推動開源策略與AI普及下，中國產業快速發展出現供給過剩，產業獲利
能力面臨挑戰，未來三年中國AI產業將可能處於虧損狀態，不過即使模型價格下降，AI使用量
增加將帶動Token需求大幅成長，其中具有企業客戶、專有資料及生態優勢的AI龍頭，將比單

純提供模型服務的公司更具長期競爭力。
黃金
• 歐美國家財政可持續性的疑慮持續發酵，加上長期通膨風險升溫，宏觀不確定性讓實體黃金
市場逆勢吸金。本波下跌主要反映去槓桿交易，實體黃金的基本面未變，有望為金價帶來中
期的支撐。建議投資人逢修正分批進場，可透過黃金存摺或是實體黃金ETF(GLD)，以投資組
合多元化的角度布局。波段投機者，從技術指標來看，可留意4,000美元關卡是否出現支撐。

美國9月ISM服務需求略降、成本壓力升高，景氣韌性與通膨風險並存
• 服務業維持擴張，需求略降但就業回穩：9月ISM服務業指數降至54.9(前月55.4)，連續27個月位於擴張區間；商業活動
指數降至56.5、新訂單降至59.8，顯示服務需求較前月放緩，但就業指數回升至50.1、重返擴張區間，訂單積壓升至
56.6，顯示內需仍具韌性，服務業尚未出現明顯轉弱訊號。
• 成本與供應鏈壓力升高，通膨降溫面臨干擾：價格指數升至74.0，創2022年7月以來新高，且為7個月來第6次高於70；
供應商交貨指數升至53.2，顯示交貨速度放緩，燃料、柴油、鋼材、銅及記憶體等成本上升，加上供應受限，使服務業
投入成本壓力擴大，通膨降溫進程面臨干擾。
• 市場觀察：美國服務業為經濟成長的重要支撐，目前經濟呈現「需求仍強、通膨升溫」的拉鋸格局，雖服務業景氣支撐

美國企業獲利，但成本上升可能推升利率波動，投資上宜偏向美國短中天期投資級債，兼顧較高收益率與利率風險控管；
股票則可聚焦具定價能力、獲利與現金流穩健的優質企業。
資料來源：Bloomberg， 2026.10.6 資料來源：ISM Services PMI Report， 2026.10.6

全球利率同步走高、AI投資持續擴張，長端美債壓力仍高
• 全球長債殖利率同步走高，反映利率環境重新定價：美國財政部長貝森特指出，近期美債殖利率攀升與德國、日本等主
要債市同步上行，反映全球資本成本重新定價，而非市場對美國資產失去信心；加上目前尚未出現資金大規模撤離美債
並轉向其他主權債券的現象，因此債市波動較偏向全球利率環境調整，而非金融系統性風險。
• AI投資增加資金需求，支撐較高利率環境：貝森特亦駁斥AI泡沫論，認為微軟、Alphabet及Meta等大型科技企業仍以
實際獲利與現金流支撐持續擴大AI資本支出，不同於過去缺乏基本面的科技泡沫；龐大的資料中心、電力與半導體投資
需求提高整體資金成本，也成為推升長天期利率的重要因素之一。
• 市場觀察：目前市場經濟近況屬於利率高檔、成長尚穩的情境中，因而投資策略建議配置大型科技股、AI基建供應鏈及

受惠利差環境的金融股具相對優勢；債券配置則以中短天期投資級債優先，避免長天期債券受利率上行壓力侵蝕。
資料來源：Bloomberg， 2026.10.6

• 美國8月貿易逆差擴大至2025年初以來最高水平：美國提高進口關稅後，貿易失衡問題仍未明顯改善，商務部數據顯示，8
月貿易逆差擴大13.7%至1,056億美元，且超出市場預期的1,021億美元。經通膨調整後，8月商品貿易逆差擴大至1,147億美
元，同樣為2025年3月以來最大。
• 觀察美國本土投資能否進一步轉化為出口能力：AI建設帶動半導體與設備進口，加上能源成本上升及關稅政策干擾，推升進
口額至歷史新高。不過，強勁內需也顯示消費與企業投資仍具支撐力。隨著第三季財報季即將展開，根據Factset數據，分析
師預期第三季標普500企業獲利年增約29.5%，加上企業獲利預期持續上修，強化投資人對高利率環境下企業基本面的信心。
投資策略上，相較於單筆投資，此時的高成長與高波動的環境，更適合分批布局的定期定額策略來參與市場。

獲利成長；中長期建議維持日股正向看法，聚焦金融、AI、機器人、自動化及電力設備等具結構性成長題材的企業。

中國AI產業陷入逐底競爭困境
• 中國AI產業陷入逐底競爭困境：彭博指出在政府積極推動開源策略與
AI普及下，中國AI產業快速發展，但也逐漸出現供給過剩，使產業獲
利能力面臨挑戰。開源模式雖有助於降低技術門檻、應用普及，但也
使技術優勢難以長期維持，加上中國市場長期習慣低價甚至免費的數
位服務，多數AI業者難以透過訂閱模式獲利，轉而依賴利潤較低的API
服務作為主要收入來源。目前中國已備案大型語言模型(LLM)數量達
988個，包括DeepSeek、Kimi及智譜AI等新創企業快速崛起，進一步
加劇市場競爭。此外API服務逐漸被視為基礎公用服務，即使AI Agent
帶動詞元(Token)需求快速成長，也未必能同步提升獲利，由於開源模
型可由不同雲端業者部署，市場進入門檻降低，業者更難建立長期競

爭優勢。
• 後市展望：考量龐大資本支出與持續價格競爭，未來三年中國AI產業
整體仍可能處於虧損狀態，市場將形成「美國閉源、高毛利」與「中
國開源、低成本」模式，即使模型價格下降，AI使用量增加將帶動
Token需求大幅成長，進而推升資料中心、雲端服務及AI晶片需求，
從投資角度來看，基礎設施供應商(晶片、伺服器、資料中心、雲端)受
惠程度可能高於模型開發商；而具有企業客戶、專有資料及生態優勢
的AI龍頭，將比單純提供模型服務的公司更具長期競爭力。
中國AI市場規模預測
中國已備案的LLM數量

人行下調抵押補充貸款利率和房貸貼息等政策出台，有望提振中國經濟
和市場信心
• 人行下調抵押補充貸款利率和房貸貼息等政策出台，有望提振中國經濟和市場信心：日前中國推出購房貸款貼息政策及下調
PSL(抵押補充貸款)利率，代表穩增長政策進一步加碼，有助於提振市場信心與經濟預期，此次政策不僅支持房地產市場，也
將「六張網」建設、科技創新、民營及小微企業，納入重點扶持範圍，透過增加再貸款額度，與提高支持比例，帶動投資、
就業及消費回升。短期A股有望在政策推動下出現築底反彈，其中基建投資、製造業投資、房市銷售及居民消費有機會改善，
同時PSL(抵押補充貸款)降息有助於流動性環境邊際寬鬆，降低市場對經濟增長的疑慮，後市來看企業獲利與信用環境有望逐
步修復，進一步鞏固A股慢牛格局。投資建議上房貸貼息政策有利房地產、建材、家居等產業鏈，金融業受惠資金成本下降與

信貸回升，食品飲料、零售、家電及服務消費則可望受惠於居民消費意願提升。

中國、日本減持美國公債的背後邏輯與差異
• 橋水基金創始人達利歐警告，美國公債正面臨中日兩國減持的風險，數據顯示這項警告已成事實。2026年前七個月，中國
美國公債持倉減少660億美元至6,180億美元；同期日本持倉亦減少820億美元至1.1兆美元，扣除價格變動的估值效應後，
兩國實質持倉均大幅下滑。然而，兩國削減美國公債的背後動機並不相同，這將導致未來的減持軌跡出現明顯分化。
• 中國戰略性長期去美元化：1.降低地緣政治風險與轉向資產多元化，隨著人民幣在國際貿易結算中使用增加，市場流動性提
升，中國對美元儲備依賴度顯著下降。2.美債財政可持續性隱憂，美國政府債務持續攀升，引發對美國長期財政健康疑慮。
3.根據彭博經濟研究模型預測，在漸進式減持情境下，中國的美元儲備(以美債與機構債為主)到2050年可能減少約50%。

• 中國搭配戰術性節奏調整(短期緩和因素)：儘管長期趨勢向下，但目前中美利差顯著(美中30年期公債利差超過350個基點)，
在美債殖利率維持高位期間，過快減持意味著實現債券價格下跌的虧損，且可能加劇人民幣升值壓力。因此，中國短中期內
傾向採取「戰略性減持、戰術性放緩」的靈活步調。
美國公債持倉：中國 vs 日本
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.10.8，美國財政部
中國預計將減少美元儲備
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.10.8，中國央行、國際貨幣基金組織、彭博經濟研究。

日本受政策利率與本土資本回流驅動
• 與中國的結構性考量不同，日本的減持主要出自週期性因素與匯率干預需求。
• 日本央行貨幣政策轉鷹：日本央行正加快升息腳步。隨著日債殖利率上揚(30年期日債殖利率升至約4.2%)，美日利差持續
收窄，吸引日本國內機構資金回流本土主權債市場。
• 匯率干預政策：日本為防範日圓過度貶值，日本財務省傾向出售部分海外美債資產以籌集外匯干預資金。若日圓再次面臨壓
力，此類拋售干預可能持續。因此，隨著日本央行逐步走向政策正常化，日本對美債的持倉大概會延續下滑軌跡。
• 小結與投資建議：美國公債作為全球無風險資產的地位正面臨結構性挑戰，中國的「長期去美元化」與日本的「本土利率回
升資金回流」將形成雙重壓力，這意味著美債殖利率短期內難大幅下行，美國財政部發債成本居高不下，進而推高全球資本

的融資成本。投資債券配置建議採取「中短天期＋階梯式配置」策略，長天期美國公債(20年以上)受發債供給過剩及中日兩
國長期減持影響，價格波動較大。建議投資人適度降低長天期美國公債暴險，轉向防禦性較佳的中短天期美國公債或高品質
高評級公司債，以鎖定當前較高殖利率同時控管利率風險。
殖利率差：美元與人民幣 vs 美元與日圓
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.9.29

黃金市場資金流向分歧，實體投資有望支撐中長期金價表現
• 利率與匯率因素，壓抑金價近期表現：9月以來，美國公債殖利率飆升、美元指數走揚，由於黃金本身不孳息，資金傾向轉
往固定收益市場，疊加美元走強壓抑國際購買意願，金價自8月底高點至今已下修約11%。
• 9月資金流向分歧，實體投資強、期貨市場弱：根據世界黃金協會統計，9月在金價下跌之際，全球實體黃金ETF卻吸引大量
資金流入，流入約100億美元(67噸)。另一方面，COMEX黃金期貨市場投機部位大幅下降，避險基金與其他機構投資人的
多頭部位明顯減少，造成期貨市場賣壓增加，進一步推動金價下跌。
• 實體黃金市場逆勢吸金反應投資人的避險需求：歐美國家財政可持續性的疑慮持續發酵，加上美伊衝突未解及AI算力需求強
勁，長期通膨風險升溫，宏觀的不確定性讓實體黃金市場逆勢吸金。從數據上來看，本波下跌主要反映去槓桿交易，實體黃

金的基本面未變，有望為金價帶來中期的支撐。建議投資人逢修正分批進場，可透過黃金存摺或是實體黃金ETF(GLD)，以
投資組合多元化的角度布局。波段投機者，從技術指標來看，可留意4,000美元關卡是否出現支撐。
資料來源：世界黃金協會，2026.10.7。

7. 本資料所載部分基金涉及非投資等級債，投資人投資以非投資等級債券為訴求之基金不宜占其投資組合過高之比重。基金經金管會核准，惟不表示絕無風險。由於
非投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，故本基金可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支
付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損。部分基金不適合無法承擔相關風險之投資人。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良
管理人之注意義務外不負責本基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
8. 基金年化配息率計算公式：每單位配息金額÷除息日前一日之淨值×一年配息次數×100%。「年化配息率」為估算值。當月配息率計算公式：每單位配息金額÷除

息日前一日之淨值×100%。
9. 基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動。投資人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績
效亦不代表未來績效之保證。本資料如以基金過去績效進行模擬投資組合之報酬率時，僅為歷史資料模擬投資組合之結果，不代表本投資組合之實際報酬率及未來
績效保證，不同時間進行模擬操作，其結果亦可能不同。
10. 部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之相關費用。基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部份，可能導致原始投資金額減損。各基金由本
金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至各投信基金公司及總代理人網站查詢。
11. 屬基金資訊觀測站ESG基金專區所列之基金，有關基金之ESG資訊，投資人應於申購前詳閱基金公開說明書或投資人須知所載之基金所有特色或目標等資訊。
警 語

## Document 062: 2026年10月貿易統計

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026年10月貿易統計.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

日本

株式会社大和総研 丸の内オフィス 〒100-6756 東京都千代田区丸の内一丁目 9番 1号 グラントウキョウ ノースタワー 
このレポートは投資勧誘を意図して提供するものではありません。このレポートの掲載情報は信頼できると考えられる情報源から作成しておりますが、その正確性、完全
性を保証するものではありません。また、記載された意見や予測等は作成時点のものであり今後予告なく変更されることがあります。㈱大和総研と大和証券㈱は、㈱大和
証券グループ本社を親会社とする大和証券グループの会社です。内容に関する一切の権利は㈱大和総研にあります。無断での複製・転載・転送等はご遠慮ください。

2026年 11月 18日 全8頁

Indicators Update 
2026年 10月貿易統計 
自動車やAI関連の輸出が堅調も、原油高で貿易赤字は継続

経済調査部 エコノミスト ビリング 安奈

[要約] 
◼ 2026年 10月の貿易統計によると、輸出金額は前年比＋20.5％と 14カ月連続で増加し、
季節調整値でも前月比＋3.3％と 2カ月ぶりに増加した。輸入金額は前年比＋21.6％と
9カ月連続で増加した（季節調整値は前月比＋3.5％）。貿易収支は▲4,765億円、季節
調整値では▲7,672億円と、ともに 6カ月連続の赤字となった。 
◼ 中東情勢を受けた供給制約は続いているものの、代替調達の進展により 10月の原油及
び粗油（以下、原油）の輸入数量は前年同月とおおむね同水準となった。だが、輸入単
価は高止まりしており、中東情勢に起因する供給制約は原油高を通じて輸入金額を押
し上げ、貿易赤字が継続している。 
◼ 輸出数量（季節調整値）は前月比＋4.0％と 3カ月ぶりに増加した。乗用車や半導体等
製造装置、非鉄金属などの増加が全体を押し上げた。地域別では、米国向け、EU向け、
アジア向けのいずれも増加した。アジア向けのうち中国向けも増加した。 
◼ 先行きの輸出数量は、横ばい圏で推移するとみている。AI関連需要などが引き続き下
支えしよう。ただし、リスク要因はAI関連投資の先行きと中東情勢だ。AI関連投資の
持続性には不透明感があり、資本財輸出の下押し要因となり得る。中東からの原油輸
出には回復の動きが見込まれているものの、中東情勢をめぐる不透明感は依然として
残る。

### Page 2

1

2 / 8

【貿易金額】自動車やAI関連の輸出が堅調も、原油高で貿易赤字は継続 
2026年10月の貿易統計によると、輸出金額は前年比＋20.5％とコンセンサス（同＋20.4％、
Bloomberg調査）とほぼ一致し、14カ月連続で増加した（図表 1）。輸出増加の主因は輸出価格
（同＋13.9％）の上昇だ。円安効果があったほか、旺盛な AI・半導体需要を背景に半導体等電
子部品や半導体等製造装置などの価格が上昇していることが寄与したとみられる。輸出の実勢
を示す輸出数量は同＋5.8％と堅調に推移した。世界的な AI需要を背景に半導体関連財の輸出
が堅調だったほか、9月に自動車の挽回生産が進んだこともあり、10月の自動車輸出は比較的
強めの動きになった可能性がある。また、燃料高を背景に燃費性能に優れているハイブリッド
車への需要の高まりも輸出増を後押ししたと考えられる。輸出金額の季節調整値は前月比＋
3.3％と 2カ月ぶりに増加した（図表 2左）。輸出価格（以下、数量と価格の季節調整値は大和
総研による）は低下した一方、輸出数量が上昇し、輸出金額を押し上げた。 
輸入金額は前年比＋21.6％と 9カ月連続で増加した（図表 1）。輸入の実勢を示す輸入数量は
同▲0.2％だった一方、輸入価格が同＋21.9％と大幅に上昇し、金額を押し上げた。輸入価格
の上昇の背景には、中東情勢による原油及び粗油（以下、原油）や液化石油ガスの価格上昇が
ある。特に、代替調達先となっている米国からの原油輸入は数量面（同＋738.2％）と価格面
（同＋384.6％）で上昇傾向が続いており、米国からの輸入金額は同＋1122.8％と高い伸び率と
なった。輸入金額の季節調整値は前月比＋3.5％と増加した（図表2右）。  
10 月の原油の輸入数量は前年比▲0.7％とおおむね前年並みとなったが、輸入価格（同＋
61.2％）が大きく上昇したことで輸入金額（同＋60.5％）が押し上げられた。こうした中東情
勢に起因する供給制約による原油価格の高騰が引き続き輸入金額を押し上げ、貿易赤字が継続
する要因となっている（図表 3）。貿易収支は▲4,765億円、季節調整値では▲7,672億円と、
ともに6カ月連続の赤字となった（図表 4）。

【輸出数量】主要国・地域向けがそろって増加 
輸出数量（季節調整値）は前月比＋4.0％と3カ月ぶりに増加した。乗用車の増加が全体を押
し上げたほか、グローバルなAI関連需要を背景に半導体等製造装置や非鉄金属も増加に寄与し
た。振れの大きい船舶も下支えした。地域別では、米国向け（同＋7.9％）、EU 向け（同＋
15.7％）、アジア向け（同＋0.5％）のいずれも増加した。アジア向けのうち中国向け（同＋
5.4％）も増加した（図表 5左）。  
米国向けは 3カ月ぶりに増加した。前月に自動車の挽回生産が進んだことや、燃料高により
ハイブリッド車の販売が好調であることから、乗用車は増加した。医薬品は前月の反動で増加
したほか、半導体等製造装置は AI需要の恩恵を受け、4カ月連続で増加した。 
EU向けは4カ月ぶりに増加した。対米輸出と同様に、乗用車の増加が大きく寄与した。EU向
けの乗用車輸出は 3カ月連続で堅調であり、ハイブリッド車への需要の高まりが反映されてい
る。二輪自動車や非鉄金属、原動機なども増加した。 
アジア向けと、そのうち中国向けはともに 2カ月連続で増加した。アジア向けの増加の主因
は、2カ月連続で振れの大きい船舶の増加だった。半導体等製造装置や乗用車、非鉄金属など
も増加した。中国向けも半導体等製造装置や乗用車、非鉄金属などが増加した。

### Page 5

5 / 8

【見通し】輸出数量は横ばい圏で推移の見込み、AI関連需要と中東情勢がリスク要因 
先行きの輸出数量は、横ばい圏で推移するとみている。AI関連需要などが引き続き下支え要
因になろう。ただし、AI関連投資の先行きには不確実性もあり、その持続性には引き続き注意
が必要だ。また、中東からの原油輸出には回復の動きが見込まれているものの、中東情勢をめ
ぐる不透明感は依然として残る。 
11 月に入ってもホルムズ海峡周辺で船舶への攻撃が続いているが1、国際エネルギー機関
（IEA）の11月石油市場リポートによると、米国とイランの軍事衝突後初めて、原油市場は供給
超過に転じる見込みである。中東からの原油輸出が回復しつつあることは好材料であるものの、
同リポートでは下振れリスクが引き続き大きいとも指摘されている2。中東情勢の緊迫化に伴う
資源価格や海上輸送コスト（運賃・保険料）の高止まりが長期化すれば、物流制約に加え、海
外経済の下振れを通じて、輸出数量の下押し要因となり得る。 
米国向けは、AI関連需要を背景とした米国の設備投資の増加が追い風となり、増加すると見
込まれる。ただし、一部のAI関連企業では収益性や財務面への懸念が広がっているほか、米中
間選挙で民主党が上下両院を奪還することが確実となったことで、AIやデータセンターをめぐ
る規制強化への懸念も高まっている。設備投資の拡大が持続するか不透明だ3。設備投資の増勢
が鈍化すれば、日本からの資本財輸出にも下押し圧力がかかる可能性がある。 
アジア向けは横ばい圏で推移するだろう。このうち中国向けはやや軟調な推移が見込まれる。
中国の 2026年 7-9月期の実質 GDP成長率は前年比＋4.5％となり、2026年の政府成長率目標で
ある 4.5％～5.0％の下限と同水準となった4。中国では内需が引き続き低迷している。景気刺
激策による下支え効果が期待されるものの、需要の弱さが日本からの輸出の逆風になる構図は
続く見込みだ。 
欧州向けは横ばい圏での推移となろう。欧州では、ドイツを中心とした財政拡張を背景に、
防衛支出やインフラ投資が景気を下支えすると見込まれる5。これが欧州経済の回復につながれ
ば、日本からの財輸出も持ち直すことが期待される。ただし、足元では長期金利の上昇が加速
しており、これにより設備投資や公共投資の拡大が抑制される恐れがある。また、エネルギー
価格の高止まりによる個人消費の下振れも重しとなり得る。

1 「中東でタンカー攻撃続く、緊張緩和期待に逆風」（ロイター、2026年11月 10日公開、同 11月 11日更
新） 
2 International Energy Agency (IEA) “Oil Market Report - November 2026”（2026年11月13日） 
3 畑中宏仁・神田慶司「ハイパースケーラーの投資動向が世界経済に与える影響」（大和総研レポート、2026
年 11月 12日） 
4 齋藤尚登「中国経済見通し：不動産不況の底打ちに期待」（大和総研レポート、2026年10月22日） 
5 橋本政彦「7-9月期ユーロ圏GDP 減速しつつも上振れ」（大和総研レポート、2026年11月2日）

## Document 081: 2026年7-9月期GDP予測(最終版) ～前期比年率▲0.4％の小幅マイナス成長を予想～

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026年7-9月期GDP予測(最終版) ～前期比年率▲0.4％の小幅マイナス成長を予想～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-11-10
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Economic Indicators

2026 年 11 月10 日

1/2 
2026 年 7-9 月期 GDP 予測(最終版) 
～前期比年率▲0.4％の小幅マイナス成長を予想～ 
経済調査部 シニアエグゼクティブエコノミスト 新家義貴 
（℡：050-5474-7490）

11 月16 日に公表される26 年７-９月期の実質
ＧＤＰ成長率を前期比年率▲0.4％（前期比▲
0.1％）と予測する。10 月 30 日の段階では前期
比年率＋0.4％（前期比＋0.1％）と小幅プラス
成長を予想していたが、その後公表された経済
指標の結果を反映し、小幅マイナス成長へと予
測値を下方修正する。 
本日公表された26 年９月分の国際収支統計の
結果を反映し、26 年７-９月期の実質輸出を前期
比＋0.1％（従来予測値：同＋1.2％）へ下方修
正する。筆者の９月分の想定と比べてサービス
輸出が大きく下振れたことが主因である。実質
輸入も前期比＋3.0％（従来予測値：同＋
3.3％）と下方修正するが、輸出の下方修正度合
いの方が大きいことから、外需の予測値を前期
比年率寄与度▲1.9％Pt（従来予測値：同▲
1.3％Pt）へ下方修正している。 
また、９月分の家計調査と家計消費状況調査
が想定よりも下振れたことを反映し、個人消費
の予測値も前期比▲0.3％と、従来予測値の同▲
0.2％から小幅下方修正した。

物価高による下押しや耐久財需要の反動に加え、天候不順の影響もあって個人消費が減少する
ことが成長率を押し下げる見込みである。特に、８～９月には長雨・大雨や台風などの天候不順
が相次ぎ、外出手控えや旅行・宿泊の見送りなどを通じて消費が下押しされたとみられる。一
方、設備投資は高い伸びとなり、景気を下支えするとみられる。設備投資の伸びには特殊要因も
（％）
実質ＧＤＰ ▲ 0.1
（前期比年率） ▲ 0.4

     　　   （うち民需） 0.1
    　　    （うち公需） 0.2

(出所）内閣府「国民経済計算」、第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所
2026年7-9月期ＧＤＰ予測

### Page 2

経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。
2/2
一部含まれているが、それを除いても堅調だったと考えられる。
７-９月期は４四半期ぶりのマイナス成長になると予想するが、マイナス幅は大きくない。今回
のマイナス成長には天候不順という一時的な下押し要因も含まれており、過去３四半期に高い成
長が続いた後であることや、企業部門の底堅さが維持されていることを踏まえれば、景気回復の
流れが途切れたと判断する必要はないだろう。
物価高による節約志向は根強く、個人消費の回復にはなお時間を要するとみられるが、設備投
資を中心に企業部門が好調さを維持している点は好材料である。家計部門が力強さを欠く一方、
企業部門が景気を下支えする構図のもと、日本経済は均してみれば緩やかな回復基調にあると判
断している。

（需要項目ごとの解説は、2026 年 7-9 月期 GDP 予測（1 次速報） ～前期比年率＋0.4％と予想。成長率は鈍化も、景気回復の流れは
崩れず～ | 新家 義貴 | 第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所（10 月 30 日発行）をご参照ください）

### Page 3

CHPT (6510.TWO)

CHPT
Valuation
We value CHPT using a PE-based approach that factors in earnings momentum and the structural shift towards high-end
testing. Our target price of NT$4,000 is derived from applying 35x to 2027E EPS, implying the company's average forward PE
since 2024 +1 SD, which we believe is justified given the company's entrenched position in the 2nm/3nm testing segment,
rising contribution from US AI GPU & AI ASIC projects, and the unique competitive advantage of its "All-in-House" vertical

integration model. With these structural drivers in place, and as we believe the market is starting to value it as an AI/HPC play
(rather than a consumer electronics stock), we expect the multiple to hold at a premium relative to its historical averages.
Risks
Our quant system rates the CHPT stock as High Risk for its share price volatility.
Key downside risks that could impede the stock from reaching our target price, and to our earnings forecasts, include: 1) margin

compression from lower-than-expected utilization rates; 2) execution delays in concentrated US AI GPU and ASIC programs;
3) intensifying competition in testing boards from global peers; and 4) structural gross margin dilution from a shift towards
high-end burn-in boards.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Analysts’ compensation is determined by Citi Research management and Citigroup’s senior management and is based upon
activities and services intended to benefit the investor clients of Citigroup Global Markets Inc. and its affiliates (the “Firm”).
Compensation is not linked to specific transactions or recommendations. Like all Firm employees, analysts receive
compensation that is impacted by overall Firm profitability which includes investment banking, sales and trading, and

principal trading revenues. One factor in equity research analyst compensation is arranging corporate access events between
institutional clients and the management teams of covered companies. Typically, company management is more likely to
participate when the analyst has a positive view of the company.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

營收走勢圖

研究員/丁宥霖 yulin.ting@uni-psg.com

優群 3Q26 獲利低於預期，主要受筆電需求疲弱、稼動率下降及原
物料成本上漲影響，預期 4Q26 營運仍將承壓。惟隨 Google 伺服
器新案及網通新產品於 2027 年放量，加上高單價新產品陸續導
入，營收與獲利可望重返成長。研究部下修優群 2026、2027 年稅
後 EPS 至 10.06 元（原估 11.31 元）及 11.83 元（原估 14.30 
元）。考量短期利空已逐步反映於股價，現價對應 2027 年本益比
僅 8.8 倍，低於歷史區間下緣，且預估殖利率約 7.6%，具備下檔
支撐及評價修復空間。研究部以 2027 年 EPS 為評價基礎，給予 
12 倍本益比，目標價 140 元，投資評等由中立調升至買進。

重點摘要： 
1. 筆電需求疲弱、稼動率下降，3Q26 獲利低於預期 
優群 3Q26 營收 9.75 億元（+2.9% QoQ、−13.8% YoY），低於原
估 10.90 億元，主因筆電客戶持續調整出貨，SO-DIMM 與 M.2 營
收分別年減 36% 及 20%，抵銷伺服器與新產品成長貢獻。受營收
不如預期、產能利用率下降及原物料成本上漲影響，毛利率降至 
46.1%（−1.5ppt QoQ、−5.1ppt YoY），低於原估 47.4%；營益率
降至 27.8%，低於原估 29.9%。稅後淨利 2.55 億元（+18.9% QoQ、
−32.8% YoY），稅後 EPS 2.84 元，低於原估 3.42 元。

表一、優群 3Q26 獲利檢討及 4Q26 獲利修正

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

2. 筆電需求持續疲弱，下修 4Q26 獲利預估 
優群 10 月營收 2.58 億元（−22.9% MoM、−25.9% YoY），主要受
筆電客戶持續調整出貨影響。預期 11、12 月出貨將較 10 月回
升，惟整體需求仍偏弱，產能利用率下降將持續壓抑毛利率表現。
考量筆電需求修正幅度超出預期，研究部下修 4Q26 營收預估至 
8.80 億元（−9.8% QoQ、−15.5% YoY，原估 10.26 億元），毛利
率由原估 47.8% 下修至 45.4%，稅後 EPS 由 2.69 元下修至 
2.02 元（−29.3% QoQ、−37.9% YoY）。

3. CSP 伺服器與網通新產品放量，2027 年營運結構持續優化 
隨 AI 推論及企業運算需求成長，伺服器對記憶體容量、頻寬及傳
輸效能的要求持續提升，帶動 DDR5 R-DIMM 朝高速化與高容量發
展。相較傳統 U-DIMM，R-DIMM 對訊號完整性及可靠度要求較高，
連接器製程與品質門檻亦隨之提升，有利高單價產品需求成長。優
群持續拓展 Meta 與 Google 等 CSP 客戶訂單，2026 年 R-DIMM 
累計出貨量已超越 2025 年全年，且高單價 R-DIMM 占比持續提
升，有助優化產品組合。其中，Google 伺服器新案預計於 1Q27 在
越南廠量產，除帶動 Long-DIMM 出貨成長，亦有助提升越南廠稼
動率並改善規模經濟，研究部預估 2027 年 Long-DIMM 營收成長 
30.9% YoY。網通方面，優群持續拓展既有筆電與伺服器以外的通
訊設備應用，新產品已於 3Q26 開始量產，預期 2027 年隨客戶訂
單增加及新應用拓展，出貨規模持續擴大。搭配 CAMM2、CCBB 等
新產品貢獻，研究部預估 2027 年 Others 營收成長 33.8% YoY，
成為營運結構轉型的重要成長來源。相較之下，傳統筆電 SO-DIMM 
營收預估衰退 2.4% YoY，顯示營運成長動能逐步由筆電轉向伺服
器與網通應用，有助降低對筆電景氣的依賴。

圖一、優群產品營收比重（2024～2027F）

資料來源：優群，統一投顧預估

### Page 3

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  3

4. 記憶體模組化與可維修設計趨勢，帶動高單價產品滲透 
隨筆電朝輕薄化、高速傳輸及可維修設計發展，連接器規格持續升
級，帶動高單價產品導入。記憶體方面，傳統 SO-DIMM 受限於模
組高度與訊號傳輸需求，部分高階筆電轉向焊接式 LPDDR 設計，
壓抑傳統連接器需求；CAMM2 則兼具低矮化、高速傳輸及模組可更
換等優勢，提供新一代記憶體連接方案。優群 CAMM2 目前月出貨
約 5～10 萬顆，HP 約 10 款高階機種正進行認證，預計 1H27 量
產，隨模組供應改善及新機種導入，可望擴大出貨規模。Type-C 方
面，隨 USB-C 成為筆電充電與資料傳輸的主要介面，接口維修成
本逐漸受到品牌重視。優群推出可獨立拆卸的兩件式 Type-C，無
須因接口損壞而更換整片主機板，單價約為傳統產品的兩倍，目前
已導入聯想機種，HP 預計於 2027 年加入。此外，Metal Bar 微
型沖壓件持續由筆電拓展至記憶體及 SiP 模組，憑藉精密沖壓與
微型化製程能力，擴大高毛利產品應用。研究部預估 2027 年 
Metal Bar 營收成長約 18% YoY，隨高單價新產品滲透率提升，有
助優化產品組合並支撐毛利率回升。

5. 筆電需求修正超出預期，下修 2026 年獲利，2027 年重返成
長 
考量筆電需求疲弱、客戶持續調整出貨，加上稼動率下降及原物料
成本上漲壓抑獲利，研究部下修優群 2026 年營收預估至 37.50 
億元（−9.6% YoY，原估 40.11 億元），其中 SO-DIMM 與 M.2 營
收分別衰退 29% 及 17% YoY，為主要拖累；毛利率由原估 47.9% 
下修至 47.0%，稅後淨利預估 9.03 億元（−21.0% YoY），稅後 
EPS 由 11.31 元下修至 10.06 元。 
展望 2027 年，隨 CSP 伺服器新案放量、網通業務擴張及高單價
新產品持續貢獻，研究部預估 Long-DIMM、Metal Bar 及其他產品
營收分別成長約 31%、18% 及 34% YoY，帶動全年營收回升至 
41.12 億元（+9.7% YoY，原估 46.67 億元）。隨營收規模擴大、
稼動率提升及產品組合改善，毛利率預估回升至 47.6%，稅後淨利 
10.54 億元（+16.8% YoY），稅後 EPS 11.83 元（+16.8% YoY，
原估 14.30 元）。

### Page 4

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  4

6. 短期利空已逐步反映，低評價與高殖利率浮現投資價值 
考量筆電需求疲弱及短期獲利下修，優群股價已大幅修正，現價對
應 2027 年本益比僅 8.8 倍，低於歷史 10～20 倍區間下緣，反
映短期營運利空已逐步消化。此外，優群近五年現金配息率約 78%
～79%，以研究部預估 2026 年 EPS 10.06 元推算，現金股利約 
7.9 元，按 11/18 收盤價 104.5 元計算，預估殖利率約 7.6%，
提供下檔支撐。展望 2027 年，隨 CSP 伺服器與網通新業務放量，
營收及獲利可望重返成長，帶動評價修復。綜合考量短期利空已逐
步反映、獲利成長動能回升及殖利率支撐，研究部以 2027 年 EPS 
為評價基礎，給予 12 倍本益比，目標價 140 元，投資評等由中
立調升至買進。

## Document 212: 6515穎崴 亞東

Source PDF: all report pdf/6515穎崴 亞東.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-19
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

◼ 投資建議: 
美系 AI GPU 與客製化 ASIC 持續開案，帶動高階測試座需求。模型預估，穎崴的 NVIDIA 
系統級測試座（SLT）營收將由 2026 年的 33.26 億元增至 2027 年的 83.45 億元，貢獻公
司逾半營收增量。公司目標於 2027 上半年將自製彈簧針月產能提升至 1,400 萬針，有助降
低外購成本，支撐全年毛利率由 40.92% 升至 42.97%。穎崴的矽光子與 CPO 測試方案已涵蓋
晶圓、封裝及模組，若客戶專案進入量產，可望帶來額外營收。 
根據模型，穎崴(6515)目標價 5,711 元。依模型預估的 2027 年 EPS 163.18 元 × 基準本益比 
35 倍估算，較 2026/11/18 收盤價 3,560 元具 60.4% 潛在上漲空間。建議買進 
◼ 未來觀察： 
1. 探針自製率&產能狀況 
2. HPC/AI 業務成長性 
◼ 公司是否涉及經營權爭奪之情事：無。 
◼ 產業概況： 
1. 半導體測試介面市場:系統測試需求高速成長。 
AI GPU、伺服器 CPU 與雲端業者自研 ASIC 擴張，帶動高階晶片封裝後最終測試（FT）與
系統級測試（SLT）需求成長。FT 把關量產電性品質；SLT 模擬實際系統與軟體運作，補足 
FT 對系統整合、長時間與極端負載之測試不足，隨晶片複雜度提升，滲透率持續走高。 
市場預估全球高階 HPC FT/SLT Socket TAM 將自 2026E 9 億美元躍升至 2028E 37 億美元，
CAGR 約 104%（SLT 達約 110%）。動能來自時間與耗材乘數效應：2025–2028E AI 晶片 
FT/SLT 測試時間 CAGR 達 15–20%（如 Rubin SLT 增至 120–150+ 分鐘），壓低單槽產出
並迫使客戶擴增平行測試槽位。隨大封裝、萬級以上高 Pin 數、高頻高速及破千瓦功耗成為
主流，高階測試座須整合訊號完整性、大電流與溫控散熱，帶動 FT Socket 單顆價值量提升 2 
至 3 倍；長時高功耗壓接亦加速探針磨損與更換，創造 4 至 5 倍耗材數量乘數效應。

### Page 2

◼ 公司簡介： 
1. 穎崴主要專注於半導體測試介面之研發設計與製造銷售，目前作為全球最大測試座
（Test Socket）供應商。主要產品涵蓋後端 FT Test、SLT Test、與 Burn-in Test 用之同軸
測試座（Coaxial Socket）、 HyperSocket、Burn-in Socket。今年與 TPI 簽訂五年合作契約
積極擴展前端晶圓測試 CP Test 用之 MEMS Probe Card，並整合主動溫控與液冷技術開
發高工耗測試逤需要的散熱測試解決方案。穎崴目前主要生產基地分布於高雄，分別為
高雄探針廠、高雄仁武廠，仁武廠包含一廠與二廠，其中仁武二廠已於 2Q27 投產。新
竹廠負責探針卡製造。 
2. 1H26 營收結構為 Coaxial Socket 48%、Probe Card 27%、PoP & Plastic Socket 14%、
Contact Element 6%、Others 3%、Burn-in Socket 2%。若按應用別區分，HPC & AI 占比
已達 70%居應用類別首位，其次 Smart Phone 8%、而 Networking／車用及其他合計 
22%。至 3Q26，依 Citi 調研，隨探針卡專案出貨階段性收尾及高階測試座放量，FT 
Test、SLT Test & Burn-in Socket 合計比重回升至 72%。其中 Coaxial Socket 預估 60%、
RF & Plastic Socket 大概 11%、Burn-in Socket 則佔 1%。至於 Probe Card 預估降至 20% 
3. 公司整體合併毛利率於 1Q26 為 43.0%、2Q26 因 MEMS 探針卡占比拉升及新產能折舊影
響降至 38.3%，3Q26 隨測試座比重提高毛利率已回升至 40.5%。各產品線方面，依摩
根大通調研，Socket 與 Contact Elements 毛利率穩定維持在 40%+。而 MEMS Probe Card 
因須外購關鍵探針元件，因此即便 2026 年生效之二期協議已改善獲利，毛利率仍僅約 
30–35%。目前穎崴 Pogo pin 自製程度高達 60%，隨自製彈簧針 pogo pin 月產能由 1H26
的 600 萬 pins 擴至 1H27 的 1,400 萬 pins，將有助進一步支撐 Socket 毛利率。 
4. 客戶比重:截至 2026 年活躍客戶超過 200 家，但營收高度集中於高階 AI、HPC、GPU、
ASIC 與伺服器 CPU 客戶。前十大客戶合計占營收比重達 90%，且 7 奈米及以下先進

### Page 3

製程產品占比高達 89%。根據摩根大通，2026 全年穎崴核心終端客戶營收占比以
Nvidia 居首，佔比大約 50–60%。其次為 AMD，預計 10–15%與 Google 約 2%。
◼ 成長動能：
1. AI GPU／ASIC 推升 SLT 與高階 FT 測試座需求)
AI 晶片朝大封裝、高 Pin 數、高功耗與高速傳輸升級，拉長測試時間並放大 SLT Test 導入
需求，使整體市場進入倍數擴張期。在 TAM 動能上，全球高階 HPC FT Test／SLT Test 測試
座市場規模預計自2026E 的 9 億美元躍升至2028E 的 37 億美元，市場預估從 2026 到 2028
相關產品的市場CAGR 將逾 100%，其中價值量更高的 SLT Test 測試座將由 7 億美元跳升至
31 億美元。隨穎崴切入 Nvidia SLT Test 供應鏈，目前市占率預估由 2026E 的 20% 升至
2028E 的30%，外加 2027 年雲端業者如 Google、AMD 的自研 ASIC 與伺服器 CPU 擴大量產，

FT Test／SLT Test 測試座占穎崴總營收比重預估將於 2027E–2028E 維持於 64%–79% 區
間。根據自有模型估算，測試座業務於 2027E 預計貢獻營收約新台幣 161 億–192 億元、
2028E 擴增至約 274 億–362 億元，整體測試座 2026–2028E 營收 CAGR 區間約為 61%–
89%。
2. HyperSocket 與液冷熱管理產品放量
下一代 AI 晶片正由約 10000 Pin 邁向20000 Pin 以上，且由於 AMD MI455、TPUv9 系列新
世代平台封裝尺寸將跨過 100×100mm 至 120×120mm 門檻，基板翹曲與大電流散熱成為測試瓶
頸。穎崴專利 HyperSocket 結合主動液冷熱管理方案可克服大尺寸封裝翹曲並維持穩定電
性，因此Pogo Pin 單針 ASP 相較既有高階同軸測試座享有約 30%溢價，帶動產品組合持續升
級，目前穎崴公司預估 HyperSocket 與液冷測試解決方案將於 2027 占總營收 5%，但考量客

戶大封裝世代切換節奏，普遍預期放量重心將落在 2028 年。依自有模型的預估，該測試解決
方案預計於2027 貢獻總營收約 3%–5%（約新台幣 9 億–15 億元），2028 有機會放大至約
5%–6%（約新台幣 21 億–32 億元），相較 2027 年呈現倍數級年增動能，並有助高階測試座
在擴產自製化下將 2027E–2028E 合併毛利率支撐於 41.1%–46.2% 區間。

### Page 4

3. 新增產能釋放與 CPO 測試
因應既有產能滿載，高雄仁武二廠已於2Q27 投產，其中新增 Socket 產能自 3 月開出並在第
二季順利認列營收，使 5,000-pin 的等效規格 Socket 月產能自 2026 下半年的 4.2k 套擴增逾
70%至 1H27 的 7.2k 套，自製 Pogo Pin 月產能亦由 900 萬 pins 增逾 50%至 1,400 萬 pins，預
估 2027 年底可達單月 1,600–1,800 萬 pins。新產能開出讓既有高階 AI 積壓的訂單順利轉
化為營收，支撐目前市場與自有模型所預估的穎崴 2027E 總營收區間達新台幣 213.5 億–
303.7 億元（YoY +45%–85%）、2028E 達 351.0 億–528.3 億元（YoY +64%–76%），對應
2026–2028E 總營收 CAGR 區間為 54%–81%（EPS CAGR 區間則達 76%–108%）。CPO 的測試
方案預計2H27 開始小量出貨，營收貢獻大約低個位數以下，至 2028 年進入量產成熟階段，

預計可以貢獻2028 年營收最高至接近 10%。
4. 1H26
1H26 合併營收 65.03 億元，HoH +61.0%、YoY +70.3%（1Q26 為 29.80 億元、2Q26 為
35.23 億元），整體毛利率為 40.5%（1Q26 為 43.0%、2Q26 為 38.3%）。稅後淨利 13.71 億
元（YoY +67.6%、HoH +60.4%；1Q26 為 6.99 億元、2Q26 為 6.72 億元），1H26 累計 EPS
達 38.24 元（1Q26 為 19.54 元、2Q26 為 18.70 元），上半年營收與獲利均創歷年同期新
高。截至最新已公告進度，累計前 9 月合併營收達 110.59 億元（年增 96.7%），同創歷年
同期新高。在產品組合與毛利率比較上，1H26 產品營收比重仍以 Coaxial Socket（48%）與
Probe Card（27%）為核心。
⚫ 與1H25（毛利率 48.9%）比較：1H26 毛利率表現明顯較差（YoY -8.4 個百分點），主

因1H25 銷售較多高毛利的 Non-MEMS Probe Card 及傳統高毛利組合，而 1H26 向
Technoprobe 外購關鍵 MEMS 探針元件的 MEMS Probe Card 營收占比擴大，上述外購元
件結構拉低 MEMS Probe Card 毛利率，使其落在 30%–35%，低於測試座及接觸元件的
40% 以上水準，對整體毛利率形成結構性稀釋。

### Page 5

⚫ 2H26E 穎崴合併營收 79.56 億元（HoH +22.3%、YoY +97.0%），毛利率 41.29%（HoH 
+0.82 ppt、YoY -0.58 ppt），歸屬母公司稅後淨利 17.71 億元（HoH +29.2%、YoY 
+107.1%），基本 EPS 48.76 元（HoH +27.5%、YoY +103.5%）。

⚫ 2026E 穎崴合併營收 144.60 億元（YoY +84.0%），毛利率 40.92%（YoY -4.34 
ppt），歸屬母公司稅後淨利 31.42 億元（YoY +87.8%），基本 EPS 87.04 元（YoY 
+85.5%）。 
5. 2026/2027 
產能與資本支出方面，仁武二廠與下一座新廠預計於 2Q27–1Q28 陸續投產，推升 2027 年探
針月產能達 1,400 萬–1,800 萬支、自製率提高至 50%–60%，5,000-pin 等效測試座月產能於 
1H27 達 7.2k 套等於穎崴整體測試座產能將在 2027 倍增，2027–2028 年度預估的資本支出介
於新台幣 20.0 億–24.0 億元，其中自有模型預估 2027 為 20.0 億到 22.0 億元之間、2028 預估
為 22.0 億到 24.0 億元之間。新產品線方面，受惠高階 Functional Burn-in 升級，2027–2028E 
Burn-in 測試座營收預估為新台幣 6.40 億–10.53 億元，2027 模型預估約 6.40 億元、2028 則
有望到 10.53 億元，占總營收約 3%成為未來關注的成長動能之一。CPO 與矽光子測試預計自 
2H27 起小量出貨，2028E 營收貢獻區間為 5%-10%不等的年營收，端看實際量產與交貨情況。。

⚫ 2027E 穎崴合併營收 238.42 億元（YoY +64.9%），毛利率 42.97%（YoY +2.05 ppt），
歸屬母公司稅後淨利 59.53 億元（YoY +89.5%），基本 EPS 163.18 元（ YoY +87.5%）。

⚫ 2028E 穎崴合併營收 407.33 億元（YoY +70.8%），毛利率 44.33%（YoY +1.36 ppt），
歸屬母公司稅後淨利 111.08 億元（YoY +86.6%），基本 EPS 304.52 元（ YoY +86.6%）。

## Document 261: [329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸HD현대중공업(329180) 3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸 - 기업

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
악조건 속에서도 이 정도 
HD현대중공업의 2026년 3분기 영업실적은 매출액 5조 7,536억원(yoy +30.2%, qoq 
`-9.1%), 영업이익 8,992억원(yoy +61.3%, qoq -13.5%)으로 당사 추정치에 대체로 
부합하였다. 조업일수 감소와 원화 강세로 매출액이 감소했으며, 임단협 타결로 
인한 성과금 및 격려금이 당분기에 비용으로 반영되었다. 해양플랜트 부문에서는 
RUYA 프로젝트의 공사 진행률 확대와 TRION 프로젝트의 C/O(계약 변경) 
180억원이 추가로 반영되며 매출액이 전분기대비 0.7% 증가하였다. 엔진기계 
부문은 환율 하락을 판가 상승과 D/F(이중연료) 비중 증가로 상쇄하며 전분기와 
유사한 영업실적을 달성했다. 
2027년에도 지속될 수주 시점 효과 
3분기 대형선 야드의 수주 시점별 매출 비중은 2022년 4%, 2023년 45%, 2024년 
31%, 2025년 20% 수준으로 파악된다. 상대적으로 선가가 낮았던 2022~2023년 
물량이 여전히 매출의 절반 가량을 차지하고 있는 셈이다. 따라서 2027년 말까지도 
고선가 물량의 매출 비중 확대가 이어질 것으로 예상된다. 이는 LNG운반선 매출 
비중 감소에도 불구하고 수익성을 방어할 수 있는 핵심 요인으로 작용할 전망이다.   
투자의견 매수 유지, 목표주가 550,000원으로 하향 
투자의견은 매수로 유지하나, 목표주가는 550,000원으로 34.5% 하향한다. 환율 
가정을 기존 1,450원 수준에서 1,400원으로 조정함에 따라 목표주가 산정의 기준이 
되는 2027~2028년 실적추정치를 하향조정하였다. 또한 미국 네오 클라우드 
업체들의 자금 조달 여건 악화로 데이터센터 증설이 지연되고 있어, 발전용 엔진 
증설을 근거로 동사에 부여하였던 프리미엄을 제거하여 P/E 배수를 19.8배로 
하향조정했다. 그러나 본업인 상선 부문은 향후 고부가가치 가스선의 발주 강세를 
전망하기 때문에 투자의견은 매수로 유지한다.

Compliance Notice 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 외부의 압력이나 부당한 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하여 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
동 자료는 기관투자가 또는 제3자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다. 
동 자료는 조사분석자료 작성에 참여한 외부인(계열회사 및 그 임직원등)이 없습니다. 
조사분석 담당자 및 배우자는 해당종목과 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 
동 자료에 언급된 종목의 지분율 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
당사는 상기 명시한 사항 외 고지해야 하는 특별한 이해관계가 없습니다.

종목명 담당자 담당자(배우자) 보유여부 1%이상 보유여부 유가증권 발행관련 계열사 관계여부 공개매수 사무취급 IPO 회사채 지급보증 중대한 이해관계 M&A 관련 수량 취득가 취득일 
해당 사항 없음

## Document 265: [329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 업종 내 여전히 매력적인 선택지HD현대중공업(329180) 업종 내 여전히 매력적인 선택지업종 내 여전히 매력적인 선택지 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 업종 내 여전히 매력적인 선택지HD현대중공업(329180) 업종 내 여전히 매력적인 선택지업종 내 여전히 매력적인 선택지 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-15
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

HD현대중공업의 3Q26 연결 실적은 매출액 5조 8,602억원(yoy +33%, qoq -7%), 
영업이익 9,161억원(yoy +64%, qoq -12%, OPM 15.6%)으로 시장 컨센서스를 하회할 
전망이다. 동사의 3Q26 조업일수는 여름휴가, 추석연휴 및 중대재해 등 영향으로 
qoq -21% 감소했으며 환율 역시 qoq -84원 감소한 영향을 감안해 전분기 대비 
실적을 보수적으로 추정했다. 그럼에도 해양 부문 Trion FPU발 C/O에 따른 일회성 
환입이 예상됨에 따라 일부 이익 상쇄가 가능할 것으로 예상된다. 한편, 동사의 
임단협 부결로 당분기 추가적인 인건비 부담은 제한적일 것으로 판단되며 잠수함 
화재 사고 관련 비용 역시 4Q 이후 반영될 전망이다.

동사는 급격히 커져가는 AIDC향 전력수요 및 발전용 엔진 수주에 대응하고자 9/10 
총 4GW 규모의 4행정 중속엔진 캐파 증설을 위한 신규 투자를 공시헸다. 동사의 
기존 DC향 4행정 중속엔진 수주 단가를 고려 시 GW당 약 1조원의 매출 창출이 
가능할 전망이며 2행정 및 선박용 4행정 엔진 대비 높은 수익성이 예상되는 바 
생산이 본격화될 28년부터 동사 실적 성장성의 핵심 동인으로 작용할 전망이다.

동사에 대해 투자의견 BUY를 유지하며 업종 밸류에이션 디레이팅 및 시장 프리미엄 
조정에 따른 Target Multipe 변동으로 목표주가를 70만원으로 하향 조정한다. 상선 
시장 수주 경쟁 심화로 업종 밸류 조정이 이루어지고 있으나 동사는 엔진 기반 장기 
성장성으로 Peer 대비 상대적 매력도 확보 및 주가 방어가 가능하다는 판단이다.

## Document 282: AI估值被严重误判！贝莱德基金经理驳斥“见顶论”：Anthropic上市标志着应用层大爆发

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI估值被严重误判！贝莱德基金经理驳斥“见顶论”：Anthropic上市标志着应用层大爆发.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-26
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

⻉莱德 AI 主题 ETF 基⾦经理 Tony Kim 认为，市场严重误判了 AI 估值：芯⽚硬件股盈利⾼增
却已跌⾄低于标普 500 的⽔平，背后是 "2027 年⻅顶、 2028 年下滑 " 的⼀致预期。模型能⼒
每年提升⼗倍、落地速度远未跟上， AI 交易正从算⼒层切向应⽤层， Anthropic 融资事件是
分⽔岭；五年后机器流量将占互联⽹ 99% ， Agent ⾝份安全与数据层是最先受益的环节。
摘要：
管理着约 140 亿美元⻉莱德 AI 主题 ETF 的 Tony Kim ，对当下华尔街最流⾏的⼀个判断给出了截然
相反的答案。
他在彭博《 Trillions 》播客中认为，市场正在为 "AI 资本开⽀ 2027 年⻅顶、 2028 年⾛平甚⾄下
滑 " 定价，直接后果是 —— 芯⽚与硬件公司的盈利在成倍增⻓，股价却在跌，估值已经低于标普
500 整体⽔平。" 我确实认为市场在向下超调。 "
在他看来，这不是估值分歧，⽽是对 " 变化速率 " 的系统性低估：模型能⼒每年提升约⼀个数量

级，⽽现实经济的采⽤速度远远落后于这条曲线。
他把 Anthropic 的⼀级市场融资事件视为分⽔岭 ——AI 交易正从 " 建算⼒ " 切换到 " 跑应⽤ " 。
芯⽚股⽐⼤盘还便宜，市场在给⼀个 "2027 ⻅顶 " 的剧本定价
Kim 的第⼀层论证从估值结构切⼊。他把当下的科技板块拆成两块：芯⽚与硬件，以及超⼤
⼚。
原因不在基本⾯，在预期。
他把这称作当前最⼤的分歧点：" 要么你相信 AI 会继续下去，那这些东西便宜得离谱；要么你相
信 AI 会⻅顶回落，那市场的恐惧就是合理的。 "
超⼤⼚同样被下杀了。 Kim 认为，⾕歌、微软、 Meta 这类公司正在把业务的根本重⼼转向算⼒
基础设施提供⽅， " 它们把每⼀块钱利润都投进资本开⽀，⾃由现⾦流因此掉下来甚⾄转负，估
值⾃然被打下来 " 。他的判断是这类公司的资本开⽀还要再烧 " 两三年 " ，之后才会迎来回报。
⾄于市场为何在此时杀估值，他归因于融资能⼒与投资回报两个担忧叠加。今年约⼀万亿美元
的资本开⽀中， "85% 到 90% 来⾃最⼤的那⼏家公司，只有 10% 到 15% 落在那些资产负债表不够

厚的第⼆梯队新云⼚商⾝上 " ，⽽这部分恰恰是市场最担⼼的环节，英伟达还是其中最⼤的⽀持
者之⼀。
" 这些公司现在的估值⽐标普 500 还便宜。我知道这听起来很离谱。它们的盈利在成倍增⻓，股
价却在往下⾛，所以市盈率越压越低。 "
" 市场认为 AI 会在 2027 年⻅顶。⽽且⼀种越来越成形的看法是， 2028 年会⾛平甚⾄下滑。 "
" 我认为市场对这⻔⽣意的融资能⼒做了过度修正。 "
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

⼗倍年增速与落地速度之间的裂缝
⽀撑  Kim 逆向判断的核⼼，是⼀个被他反复强调的概念：变化的速率。
他提到，随着 " 递归⾃我改进 " 这类机制出现，这条曲线的斜率还可能更陡。但真正的⽭盾在于另
⼀端：能⼒在⻜跃，采⽤在爬⾏。
这条裂缝正是他解释 " 看不到 ROI" 质疑的钥匙： " ⼤家说这项投资看不到回报，我认为它最终会显
现出来，只是也许不是现在，是明年、后年。 "
同样的逻辑也解释了为什么他期待应⽤层。Kim 把 AI 拆成两层：⼀层是 " 原⼦ "—— 电⼒、芯
⽚、服务器、云、超⼤⼚构成的计算与基础设施层；另⼀层是 " 电⼦ "—— 软件与服务构成的应⽤
层。过去四年，全部故事都发⽣在第⼀层。
Agent 数量将百倍于⼈⼝，安全与数据是最先被点燃的环节
Kim 对应⽤层最先落地的⽅向，给出了⼀个反直觉的排序：⽹络安全。
他持仓中的  Okta 是典型案例。这家做⾝份安全的公司，核⼼业务是为⼈类提供⾝份认证，⼏年
前⾼增⻓，之后增速回落到个位数， "AI 来了之后， 2023 到 2025 年这块核⼼业务并没有加速 " 。

转折发⽣在今年。
他给了⼀个量级对⽐：地球上有 80 亿⼈，⽽机器⾝份的数量最终可能是 " 上千亿 " 。
第⼆条线索是数据层。Kim ⽤⼀句话概括了他眼中未来企业的形态：
" 模型能⼒⼤概⼀年提升⼀个数量级。如果算⼗年，就是⼀万倍；再⼗年，⼜是⼀万倍。这是⾮
线性的。 "
" 模型能⼒的增⻓速度，快于它在现实经济中的扩散速度。 "
" 这次 Anthropic 的上市，第⼀次出现⼀家纯粹的应⽤公司，也许它会开启 AI 的下⼀个阶段，让我
们⾛得⽐算⼒更宽。 "
" 现在有了 Agent ，它们不是⼈，是机器。今年出了这么多安全事件，从 Hugging Face 那次攻击
开始，⼤家意识到这些 Agent 可能会失控。所以你需要的不只是⼈类⾝份的安全，还有机器⾝
份、 Agent ⾝份的安全。 "
" 这个变化今年才刚刚开始，还处在极早期的阶段。 "
" 你会接⼊智能，选⼀个模型；中间是彭博、⻉莱德这样的数据层；上⾯跑着 Agent 。未来的公司
就⻓这样， Agent 和你的数据交互，你往⾥灌原始智能。 "

### Page 3

沿着这个图景，⻉莱德在数据管道、 Agent 构建、 Agent 防护三个环节上布了仓。他把这称作 " 企
业整体重构的第⼀局 " 。
99% 的流量将由机器产⽣，⽽市场还在赌算⼒⻅顶
Kim 引⽤了  Cloudflare 的数据：今年年初， AI 与机器流量已经超过⼈类流量。
他的推演是：每个⼈未来会有⼗个、甚⾄上百个 Agent 昼夜不停地⼯作。⼈类的刷⼿机流量在总
量⾥会变得微不⾜道，⽽更耗算⼒的是那些⾮⼈类的负载 —— 药物研发、材料发现、化学与⽣
物学、机器⼈系统训练， " 可能会有⼀整个数据中⼼只做药物发现 " 。
这正好回到他对市场定价的批评：
⾄于 AI 交易的新阶段怎么布局， Kim 的判断是软件、服务与 AI 模型将三合⼀， " 以结果的形式交
付 " 。
他以⾃⼰的⼯作流举例：⽇历、邮件接⼊ AI 助⼿，每天处理两千封邮件、按优先级分流，再分设
多个 Agent 盯半导体、数据中⼼、基因组学，每周⽣成⼀份 "AI 在⽣物学领域的进展 " ⻓报告。

" 如果今天机器和⼈类流量是六⽐四，五年后会是 99 ⽐ 1 。 "
" 越来越多的算⼒负载不是由⼈产⽣的，事实上 99% 都将是⾮⼈类的。 "
" 市场在说算⼒会在 2028 年⻅顶并下滑。这与即将到来的东西完全相反 —— 所需的算⼒会⽐我们
现在设想的还要⾼出好⼏个数量级。这就是最⼤的分歧所在。 "
" 把 AI 模型、软件、服务这三样合成⼀个东西，以结果交付。然后把这个想法套⽤到标普的每⼀
个⾏业上。 "
" 现在还做不到让它替我把税报了，但⽅向是这样。 "

## Document 455: Tracking Europe’s AI transition_ A new STOXX 600 earnings-call framework (2026-11-11)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Tracking Europe’s AI transition_ A new STOXX 600 earnings-call framework (2026-11-11).pdf
Report source: DB Research
Date: 2026-11-11
Pages: 16
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Deutsche Bank 
Research Institute

Tracking Europe’s AI transition:  
A new STOXX 600 earnings-call 
framework

1 
Authors 
Michael Kirker 
Economist

November 11, 2026 
How quickly is AI moving from experimentation into everyday business, and 
where are companies reporting tangible gains? The answers matter for 
institutional investors and policymakers assessing productivity, operating 
efficiency and labour demand. Yet comparable evidence across Europe remains 
limited, making it difficult to track the pace and scale of the transition. 1 
We address this gap with a proprietary framework that turns what is said on 
STOXX 600 earnings call into three structured indicators: AI adoption 
intensity, reported productivity benefits, and the impact on workforces. 
Large language models analyse what management teams say about AI’s 
operational role, applying a common scoring framework across companies, 
sectors and countries. Our new approach provides a timely pan-European 
comparable tracker that captures not just whether AI is being mentioned, but its 
real-world impact as the adoption of the new technology unfolds. 
Key Takeaways: 
— Adoption is broadening, with signs of faster progress. AI-use scores rose 
gradually from a low base before strengthening over the past 12 months. The 
increase extends across sectors, although the pace and level of adoption 
remain uneven. 
— Workforce impacts remain limited in corporate disclosures. The 
framework does not show a broad negative workforce signal attributable to 
AI. The aggregate score remains close to the level of no impact on the labour 
force across all of our sample. However, this does not rule out AI displacing 
workers at a future date. 
— Geographic differences persist within the listed-company sample. Since 
2022, German companies in the STOXX 600 have generally recorded above-
average AI-use and reported productivity scores. Dutch and Spanish 
companies in the STOXX 600 have tended to score lower on AI use, although 
Spain has recently gone through a rapid catch up. 
— The technology J-Curve: The findings are consistent with an early stage of 
the AI transition. Companies report increasing adoption of the new 
technology, but transformational productivity gains and extensive workforce 
restructuring remain uncommon.

1 1 Our US team regularly publish on data from the USCB showing the impact of 
AI on businesses, and our German team have recently begun their own 
chartbook. In the UK, the Office for National Statistics publishes on the topic too.

### Page 2

Deutsche Bank AG Page 2

Methodology 
Sample period: Our sample begins in January 2022, providing a baseline before 
ChatGPT’s launch on 30 November 2022. AI in this framework covers both 
established and emerging technologies, so the launch of ChatGPT is a reference 
point rather than the start of all corporate AI adoption. 
Timing of observations: STOXX 600 companies report on different financial 
calendars and at quarterly or semi-annual intervals. Their earnings calls also mix 
discussion of completed activity with forward-looking guidance. 
We assign each transcript to the calendar quarter in which the earnings call takes 
place, regardless of the financial reporting period discussed. The resulting 
indicators therefore track when information about AI use and its operational 
impacts becomes public rather than when implementation or the reported effect 
occurred. This provides a common dating convention across the sample while 
retaining differences in reporting frequency and disclosure timing.

Figure 1: Coverage of the STOXX 600 earnings calls

Source: Deutsche Bank Research. Notes: Share of the STOXX 600 is based on the current company weight in the index. Data collection for the current quarter (Q4 2026) is incomplete 
as some companies are still to report

Constructing the STOXX 600 AI corpus: We use a specialised AI transcription 
engine to process earnings calls from STOXX 600 companies, restricting the 
analysis to calls for which we obtain complete transcripts of sufficient accuracy. 
In Q3 2026, the latest full quarter, the dataset covered more than 500 companies, 
representing approximately 90% of the index’s current market capitalisation 
weights (Figure 1). In the current quarter (Q4) earnings calls are still taking place. 
Nearly 250 companies with an equivalent weight to around c.60% of the STOXX 
index having already reported. 
Historical coverage typically ranges from 300 to 500 companies and represents 
more than 80% of the index’s current market-capitalisation weights. Coverage 
peaks twice a year because some European companies report semi-annually.  
11 November 2026 
Focus Europe

### Page 3

Deutsche Bank AG Page 3

### Page 4

Deutsche Bank AG Page 4

For examples of the AI’s scoring and justifications, see the tables presented in the 
Annex at the end of this report. 
Summary statistics 
Figure 2 shows the distribution of scores across all earnings-call transcripts in the 
historical sample, without weighting for company size or reporting frequency. 
These are transcript-level distributions rather than shares of companies, and they 
combine early and recent observations. They provide context for the quarterly 
indicators: 
• AI use score: Approximately 60% of earnings call transcripts do not 
discuss AI use. Among the minority that do discuss it, most fall into the 
“Active implementation” range (a score of 4-6 out of 10). 
• AI’s productivity impact: Nearly 70% of earnings calls do not mention a 
tangible AI-related productivity benefit. Among calls that do, scores are 
generally in the medium-impact band, indicating notable operational 
improvements. Transformational gains, scored at 8 or above out of 10, 
are rarely reported. 
• AI’s labour impact: Only around 10% of earnings calls discuss AI-related 
workforce adjustments. Reported changes typically receive scores of 1–
2 in magnitude on the −5 to +5 scale, with a slight tilt towards hiring and 
upskilling rather than redundancies.

Figure 2: Raw distribution of scores across the entire sample

Source: Deutsche Bank Research. Note: Results are based on the raw number of transcripts, and are independent of firm size, and biased towards firms reporting more often.

Taken together, the distributions are consistent with an early adoption phase: 
companies discuss AI implementation more often than transformational 
productivity gains or extensive workforce restructuring. A technology J-curve 
offers one interpretation of this pattern, with investment and organisational 
adaptation preceding broader benefits.  
This J-curve pattern is strongly corroborated by external micro-data, such as the 
Banco de España’s business activity survey (EBAE) of Spanish firms, which 
11 November 2026 
Focus Europe

### Page 5

Deutsche Bank AG Page 5

similarly indicates that AI has a more immediate impact on upfront investment 
and productivity than on headcount. 
AI use: A broad-based rise, with signs of faster recent adoption 
The new AI-use indicator points to a broadening transition across STOXX 600 
companies, with early signs that adoption has accelerated recently. Scores rose 
only gradually from 2022 and 2024, but the latest observations suggest a 
stronger pace across industries and country groups.  
The rise is broad-based rather than confined to one sector. Technology has led 
much of the sample, while financials show tentative recent outperformance and 
utilities have caught up from a low starting point. Among the large country groups, 
German companies have persistently recorded above-average scores.  
AI use across European industries 
To trace sectoral dispersion, we group companies by STOXX industry 
classification and calculate a weighted average of their scores for each calendar 
quarter, using their latest STOXX 600 index weights. 2  
We focus on four sectors spanning different business models: financials, the 
largest services sector; industrials, the largest physical-assets sector; technology, 
a likely early adopter; and utilities, an infrastructure-heavy sector where adoption 
may proceed more slowly.  
Figure 3 presents the AI use score for these four key industries alongside the 
range for all industry scores (represented by the shaded area). 
• The AI use score is generally low over time but is gradually rising from 
around 1 point in 2022 to around 3 in 2026. 
• AI-use scores are rising across industries, but differences between 
sectors are widening. The common direction therefore points to 
broadening adoption rather than uniform progress: sectors can 
participate in the transition while remaining at different levels of use. 
In terms of the industries of interest: 
• Technology’s early lead has narrowed. The sector’s AI-use score was 
near the top of the industry range through to 2024 but has recently 
plateaued as the broader STOXX 600 average caught up during 2025–
26.3 
• Financials and industrials broadly track the index average. Both sectors 
have remained close to the aggregate AI-use score over history, with 
financials generally a little below average, and industrials a little above. 
But financials show tentative signs of moving above average in recent 
quarters.

2 The industries are: (1) Basic Materials, (2), Consumer Discretionary, (3), Consumer Staples, (4) Energy, (5) 
Financials, (6) Health Care, (7) Industrials, (9) Real Estate, (10) Technology, (11) Telecommunications, (12) 
Utilities. 
3 Q4 2025 saw a large increase in the AI use score for the “Technology” industry, jumping up to around 6. This 
one-off spike was driven by ASML’s announcement of a significant strategic partnership with and investment 
in Mistral AI” to 'embed AI across our entire holistic portfolio in order to increase the performance and 
productivity of our systems and the yield of our customers' processes'. Because of ASML’s large market cap 
(around 49% of our Technology category), it distorts the weighted average significantly. 
11 November 2026 
Focus Europe

### Page 6

Deutsche Bank AG Page 6

• Utilities have caught up from a low base. After recording lower AI-use 
scores than other sectors for much of the sample, utilities converged 
towards the STOXX 600 average during 2026. The result shows that the 
rise in reported adoption extends beyond the sectors most closely 
associated with AI.

Figure 3: AI use score (0-10) by industry

Source: Deutsche Bank Research. Notes: The value for each industry is computed as a weighted average of all firms within the industry where the weights are derived from the current 
STOXX 600 share of each company. The markers denote the readings for the current incomplete quarter

As a cross-check, our findings align with the European Commission’s October 
Eurobarometer Flash survey of MSEs and Mid-Caps and the Eurostat annual ICT 
surveys, which show low overall intensity but a similar expansion in service- and 
tech-related sectors.  
AI use across countries 
Companies in the STOXX 600 tend to be large multi-nationals. That makes it 
difficult to pin down where physically the AI transformation is taking place within 
their organisation. Instead, our country comparison is done using two 
classifications of a company’s country: 
• Country of domicile: AI is a transformation technology, and this type of 
change is likely to be driven in organisation from the top down. We 
therefore consider a company’s country as that in which it is domiciled 
(which is likely to be where the headquarters is also). 
• Country of primary listing: While companies can generally list in other 
countries, there tends to be a home bias in their choice of exchange.  
Figure 4 presents the AI use intensity metric by both of these measures of country 
(the company’s domicile and primary exchange listing). Again, the shaded area 
shows the range across all classifications, with the largest countries/exchanges 
singled out. 
German-domiciled companies and the Xetra-listed (Frankfurt stock exchange) 
group record above-average AI-use scores in most quarters since 2022.  
Spain’s AI-use score has risen noticeably over recent quarters and now exceeds 
the aggregate average, reversing its earlier below-average position.  
11 November 2026
Focus Europe

### Page 7

Deutsche Bank AG Page 7

AI-use scores rise across country groups, with indications of faster growth from 
2025. The shared direction suggests a broad European listed-company trend, 
even though differences in score levels and timing persist between countries.

Figure 4: AI use intensity (0-10) by country

Source: Deutsche Bank Research. Notes: The markers denote the readings for the current incomplete quarter

As a geographical cross-check, our index matches the broad trends of the 
Eurostat ICT annual survey, which places Germany as a clear frontrunner among 
its large peers. But some differences do exist. For instance, Eurostat shows the 
Netherlands with a very high AI adoption. As Figure 5 demonstrates, there is a 
positive correlation between the AI use score metric derived here, and that from 
Eurostat, even though the Eurostat data measures if firms are adopting AI, and 
our new use measure also incorporates the concept of how intensively firms are 
using AI.

Source: Deutsche Bank Research, Eurostat. Notes: each dot represents a country-year pair of AI usage>0

AI productivity impact: Reported benefits are rising, but 
transformational gains remain uncommon 
Higher intensity of use does not necessarily mean firms are benefiting more from 
AI. But our AI productivity impact metric does show a similar pattern to our AI 
usage metric, although it tends to be slightly lower, indicating that companies 
may not yet be reaping the full rewards for their AI investment. 
Financials stand out more clearly on reported productivity benefits than on AI-use 
intensity, while German companies again record relatively strong scores, but only 
in more recent history.

Figure 6: AI's impact on productivity (0-10) by industry

Source: Deutsche Bank Research. Notes: The markers denote the readings for the current incomplete quarter

AI productivity impact across European industries 
Reported AI-productivity benefits have become more prominent across 
industries. Figure 6 shows scores rising gradually during 2022–25, followed by 
11 November 2026 
Focus Europe

### Page 9

Deutsche Bank AG Page 9

indications of a faster increase from late 2025. The profile resembles the AI-use 
trend in Figure 4, suggesting that the stronger adoption narrative is accompanied 
by more discussion of operational gains. 
The aggregate trajectories of the AI use and AI productivity impact scores are 
similar, but sector rankings differ. Technology’s early relative strength in AI use is 
not matched by its productivity score, while financials report above-average 
productivity benefits despite more modest use scores. 
AI productivity impact across countries 
Figure 7 plots the self-report impact of AI on company productivity by country. 
Between 2022-23, German companies recorded average productivity impact, 
even though their AI use score was relatively high. However more recently, their 
AI impact score has persistently been above average. The UK grouping shows 
persistent early outperformance in AI impact during 2022–23, followed by 
convergence towards the aggregate average.

Figure 7: AI's impact on productivity (0-10) by country

Source: Deutsche Bank Research. Notes: The markers denote the readings for the current incomplete quarter

Figure 8 shows that there is a strong positive correlation between the AI use score 
and the AI productivity score across the STOXX 600 (r-squared = 0.83). The 
relationship between the scores is approximately linear, with a slope of around 
11 November 2026

### Page 10

Deutsche Bank AG Page 10

0.66: a one-point higher use score is associated with roughly a 0.66-point higher 
productivity score for each country-year.

Figure 8: Productivity and use scores are correlated, but less than 1:1

Source: Deutsche Bank Research

AI labour force impact: On net, AI may be benefiting 
employment, for now… 
Across industries and countries, the recorded score for AI’s impact on the labour 
force remains relatively small. On average, the reported impact on companies’ 
employment has been fairly neutral, with a marginal negative bias over the last 
year or so.

Figure 9: AI's impact on the labour force (-5 to +5) by industry

Source: Deutsche Bank Research. Notes: The markers denote the readings for the current incomplete quarter. Negative (positive) scores are associated with job losses (gains) 
associated with a company's AI use.

11 November 2026 
Focus Europe

### Page 11

Deutsche Bank AG Page 11

AI labour force impact across European industries 
Despite rising productivity, AI has not triggered the negative employment shock 
feared by the public. Aggregate labour impact scores across nearly all sectors 
hover near zero, with a slightly positive balance over history, and a slightly 
negative balance more recently in 2025-26 (Figure 9). But overall the magnitude 
of AI’s impact on labour remains low. For the STOXX 600 aggregate average, the 
score’s maximum deviation from 0 (no impact) is 0.43 on our 0-5 scale. 
AI labour force impact across countries 
Workforce-impact scores are also small across the main country groupings, with 
most countries slowing a slight negative tilt in recent years (Figure 10).

Figure 10: AI impact on the labour force (-5 to +5) by country

Source: Deutsche Bank Research. Notes: The markers denote the readings for the current incomplete quarter. Negative (positive) scores are associated with job losses (gains) 
associated with a company's AI use.

The lack of significant impact so far from AI on companies’ labour forces so far is 
consistent with results from our previous work looking at the other side of the 
labour market. Data from our dbDataInsights household survey showed that 
workers across Europe reported a fairly low level of fear about losing their job to 
AI over the next year. The analysis in that report found results that were more 
consistent with workers adapting to the new AI world by upskilling and 
11 November 2026 
Focus Europe

### Page 12

Deutsche Bank AG Page 12

demanding higher wages. Those results would not be consistent with a world in 
which AI was actively replacing labour. 
These findings of a low impact on labour are highly consistent with recent 
academic research (e.g., Yotzov et al., 2026), which found that nearly half of 
surveyed UK and German firms reported absolutely no impact on headcount from 
AI over the past three years, 
Conclusion 
The newly developed framework provides a recurring way to monitor company 
disclosures about AI’s impact on productivity and labour. The central finding of 
this report is a broadening AI transition rather than clear evidence of an 
economy-wide productivity breakthrough. The new metrics indicate that AI use 
remains limited but is rising across the European countries and industries 
represented in the sample. Companies report low but growing productivity gains 
and little aggregate workforce impact so far. This pattern is consistent with a 
technology J-curve, with firms still integrating the new technology, rather than 
proof that this trajectory will unfold. Limited reported workforce effects today do 
not rule out subsequent layoffs or wider organisational restructuring. 
Quarterly updates to this framework will track Europe’s AI transition and provide 
timely signals of whether companies are reporting more substantial productivity 
and employment effects. 
Beyond AI, the framework could be adapted to extract signals about employment 
decisions, investment decisions and the general economic outlook from earnings 
calls. These potential extensions could form the basis of alternative nowcasting 
models for important macroeconomic data; they are applications that will be 
explored in future work.

Transitioning to mid-cycle 
• We now favour a more balanced stance between stocks and bonds. Near-
term event risks and high earnings expectations prompt us to trim equities to a 
Core holding. The US and Asia ex-Japan (AxJ) remain our preferred regions. 
Within AxJ, we upgrade India to Overweight. 
• Higher bond yields are improving the outlook for future returns and offering 
increasingly attractive income opportunities. We are Overweight Emerging 
Market (EM) USD bonds and favour corporate over government bonds, but limit 
duration to 3-7 years. 
• We remain Overweight gold. Prices are likely to grind higher as near-term 
headwinds from higher bond yields are offset by EM central bank demand. 
Great expectations 
Individual risk events, high earnings expectations and a higher cost of capital 
point to a more moderate return environment ahead. In the very near term, 
financial markets must navigate key event risks, such as (i) the US mid-term elections, 
which have historically been associated with elevated market volatility and (ii) renewed 
geopolitical tensions, with several reports suggesting the US is considering renewed 
strikes on Iran. These concerns have pushed the Brent oil price well above USD 
110/bbl despite significant ongoing crude oil flows through the Strait of Hormuz.  
Higher bond yields combined with high earnings expectations raise the risk of 
an expectations reset. The US Q3 earnings season is attracting significant attention 
given high earnings expectations, particularly for the AI sector. However, higher US 
bond yields may start to bite as debt-financed capex takes on a greater share of total 
capex over the coming year. This, combined with already-high expectations, raises the 
risk of disappointment and a near-term repricing of expected earnings growth pace.  
Despite the current challenges, healthy growth continues to underpin the 
economic backdrop. The US economic outlook remains well supported, and the 
absolute level of year-ahead earnings expectations is high. We do not expect Fed rate 
hikes to result in overly restrictive policy. Our caution, thus, stems from the risk of an 
expectations repricing rather than an outright economic slowdown.

A more balanced stock-bond mix 
We trim global equities to a Core holding, leaving us 
Neutral on both equities and bonds and Overweight gold. 
The concerns laid out earlier point to near-term volatility risks 
and a somewhat lower long-term growth trajectory for 
equities. Our quant model also reflects this cautious outlook, 
having downgraded equities to a level usually associated with 
flat-to-modestly-negative returns. 
From a relative perspective, though, the higher yields on 
offer from bonds also raise the bar by presenting an 
increasingly compelling return outlook after several 
years of very low yields. We believe this argues for portfolios 
that are increasingly balanced between equities and bonds. 
Ways to diversify equity exposure 
We continue to favour global equity market 
diversification, with a preference for US and AxJ equities. 
We remain Overweight US and AxJ equities, expecting them 
to outperform other major regions. Earnings growth remains 
the main driver of this view – while growth rates could 
decelerate somewhat, we still expect them to remain higher 
than those of other major regions.  
Japan and Euro area equities remain attractive as 
regional diversifiers. Our positive view on both markets’ 
financial sectors supports our Core holding view on them.   
Within Asia, we are Overweight Taiwan and India. Indian 
equities have lagged the region this year, in part due to limited 
listed AI-related exposure. However, we believe this is 
increasingly likely to become an advantage as investors seek 
greater diversification away from excessive concentration in 
the AI theme. This comes at a time when the economic and 
earnings growth outlook for Indian equities is starting to 
improve. We retain a more Neutral view on Chinese equities 
amid a still-challenging growth outlook. 
We also favour greater sector diversification. In the US, 
we favour diversifying across our Overweight sectors of 
technology, financials, materials and healthcare. We also 
favour financials in Japan and the Euro area.  
Changes to our equity opportunistic ideas also reflect a 
more defensive stance. In recent weeks, we have closed our 
global semiconductors, MSCI Taiwan, US communication 
services and global power & electrification ideas and have 
opened new ideas on US healthcare and MSCI Switzerland. 
Riding the yield train 
We believe bond markets offer an attractive opportunity 
to lock in yields. The 10-year US government bond yield is 
now in a range last witnessed in 2007. While the process of 
rising yields results in lower bond prices – as painfully 
experienced in recent months – the yield level itself starts to 
significantly improve the outlook for future returns. 
Favour corporate over government bonds, but check 
your duration. Still-high credit quality means that we continue 
to favour taking on credit risk to earn a higher yield (EM USD 
bonds are preferred, but we also see investment-grade (IG) 
and high-yield (HY) bonds as Core holdings). However, 
limiting duration to 3-7 years remains key to avoiding 
excessive exposure to any inflation surprises. 
Changes to our bond opportunistic ideas also reflect a 
more balanced stance. We close our EU bank contingent 
convertible bonds (CoCos2 – please refer page 27) and US 
utilities corporate hybrids ideas. We also close our 
opportunistic idea on US 10-year government bonds following 
the recent pullback in yields, locking in gains. 
Alternatives and gold to remain relevant 
Alternatives are an effective tool for portfolio risk 
management. Hedge funds and private markets provide a 
differentiated return stream, while rotating into alternatives 
helps reduce equity beta exposure. We examine downside 
capture and how beta is reduced via hedge fund sub-
strategies and private markets in our asset allocation section.  
Gold remains a key Overweight. We trim our three- and 12-
month forecasts modestly, but see gold forming a strong base 
above USD 4,000/oz. Rising bond yields and the USD were 
recent headwinds, but we expect a softer USD and continued 
EM central bank demand to result in 12-month gains.

### Page 7

Global Market Outlook 7

Key themes

Core scenario (soft landing, 45% probability): An economic soft landing remains our core scenario, with the global economy 
overcoming this year’s oil price shock thanks to a surge in AI investment in the US and Asia. Record wealth generation driven 
by the stock market rally is supporting US consumption. Fiscal easing in the US, Germany and Japan is supporting growth. 
Meanwhile, inflation has reaccelerated due to a rebound in fuel prices as the Middle East conflict drags on. Against this backdrop, 
the Fed, ECB and BoJ all hiked rates in the same month (in September) for the first time. Although all three subsequently paused 
in October, we expect the Fed to hike twice more, the ECB once more and the BoJ thrice more by June 2027. Nevertheless, we 
expect the energy and tariff effects on inflation to fade by H2 2027, enabling the Fed and the ECB to cut rates by Q3 2027. 
Meanwhile, China is likely to target credit easing and accelerate fiscal spending in H1 2027 to revive domestic growth.  
Upside risk (no landing, 30% probability): We maintain the probability at 30% as AI-driven investment growth continues. 
There is a reasonable chance that lower oil prices next year, the AI-fuelled wealth boom and fiscal easing in Germany and Japan 
will boost ‘animal spirits’. A potential global defence spending boom, led by the US and Europe, could also lift global growth. 
Downside risk (25% probability): We raised the risk of a recession to 15%, especially if central banks are forced to accelerate 
the pace of rate hikes to douse rising inflation. A stock market downturn negating the wealth effect or a bond sell-off due to fiscal 
concerns are other tail risks. We cut stagflation probability to 10% as we see low risk of oil prices sustaining above USD 120/bbl.

Key chart  
The US-led AI-focused 
investment boom has lifted 
business confidence. The 
resulting equity market 
rally has boosted US 
household wealth, 
supporting consumption 
 Fig. 8 Robust business confidence amid AI boom; equity rally lifts US household net worth 
Purchasing managers indices; US household net worth as percentage of personal disposable income

Source: Bloomberg, Standard Chartered

Policy rates watch  
We expect the Fed to hike twice more by Q2 2027, then 
ease in H2 2027, as AI-led expansion continues. The US 
economy remains firmly in expansion. In Q3, annualised GDP 
growth accelerated to 3.4%, led by AI investments and 
consumption. In October, the composite Purchasing 
Managers’ Index (PMI) remained strong at 57.6. Since our last 
monthly report, the consensus estimate for 2026 GDP growth 
has been nudged up to 2.3% from 2.1%, and the 2027 
estimate has risen to 2.2%. That matches the Fed’s upgraded 
growth projection of 2.3% for 2026, but remains below the 
Fed’s 2.4% estimate for 2027. AI investment, fiscal support 
and household wealth are the main growth drivers, although 
consumer activity lags business spending. Higher bond yields 
and elevated oil prices are headwinds, but recession risks 
look contained. Our core scenario remains a ‘soft landing’, at 
45%, with a meaningful 30% chance of a ‘no-landing’ upside. 
Rising gasoline and diesel prices pushed US core personal 
consumption expenditures (PCE) inflation to 0.3% m/m and 
3.1% y/y, matching estimates. Consensus sees core PCE 
inflation slowing from 3.2% in Q4 2026 to 2.4% by Q4 2027 
as energy and tariff effects fade. Underlying price momentum 
is softening, and recent PCE methodology revisions have 
lowered core inflation. However, fuel costs passed on to wider 
consumers and AI-driven chip shortages remain upside risks. 
In October, the Fed held rates at 4.0%, after hiking in 
September for the first time since 2023. We expect the Fed to 
hike rates by 25bps in December and 25bps more in H1 2027 
due to elevated near-term energy inflation. It is likely to follow 
with a 25bps cut to 4.25% in Q3 2027 as disinflation returns. 
This aligns with the Fed’s projected policy path, although it 
implies a more dovish trajectory than money markets, which 
are pricing another 56bps of hikes over the next 12 months. 
Political uncertainty after the mid-term election is a key risk.

6 Macro overview – at a glance

Rajat Bhattacharya

### Page 8

Global Market Outlook 8 
ECB to hike once more in December, then pause. The 
Euro area recovery is firming. GDP grew 1.2% y/y in Q2 2026, 
up from 0.6% in Q1, and the manufacturing PMI stayed robust 
at 52.4 in October. Consensus 2026 growth has been raised 
to 1.0% from 0.9%, and 2027 is unchanged at 1.3%. The main 
supports are resilient exports and German infrastructure and 
defence spending. However, cautious consumers and soft 
private investment limit the recovery, and energy costs are 
squeezing household incomes and business margins. 
Recession is not the central expectation. French fiscal stress 
and sustained high energy costs remain the key risks. 
Higher energy prices pushed Euro area headline inflation up 
to 3.8% y/y in September from 3.2% y/y in August, while core 
inflation rose to 2.5% y/y. Consensus expects headline and 
core inflation to ease to 2% y/y and 2.4% y/y by Q4 2027, 
respectively. Encouragingly, energy has had limited impact on 
core prices, and longer-term inflation expectations remain 
stable at around 2.1%. Together, these should allow inflation 
to moderate in 2027. The main risk is that energy, and 
especially gas, prices stay high for longer than expected. 
The ECB held its deposit rate at 2.5% in October after raising 
it by 50bps this year. Inflation data continue to favour an 
additional hike in December. We expect an ECB rate hold in 
H1 2027, followed by a cut by Q3 2027 as disinflation returns. 
This policy path is more dovish than money markets, which 
are pricing more than 60bps of hikes over the next 12 months. 
China to provide targeted support to defend growth floor. 
China's growth momentum recovered in September. Both the 
manufacturing PMI (50.1) and the non-manufacturing PMI 
(50.2) moved back into expansion. The construction PMI rose 
above 50 for the first time this year as infrastructure spending 
started to take effect. Exports grew 36.3% y/y, pushing the 
trade surplus to a record USD 133.5bn. We expect growth in 
2026 to meet the lower end of the 4.5-5.0% target. Growth is 
increasingly bifurcated, though, with technology firms and 
exporters doing well, while household demand stays weak. 
Prices are rising again, but only narrowly. Consumer inflation 
edged up to 0.9% y/y in September, and producer inflation 
surged to 5% y/y, its highest since 2022, driven by energy and 
metals prices and concentrated in upstream sectors. With 
domestic demand weak and a high comparison base next 
year, the boost from energy prices is likely to fade in 2027. 
Policy is in ‘targeted easing’ mode. We expect a modest fiscal 
package to defend the growth floor rather than a large 
stimulus. The PBoC cut a key rate by 25bps to 1.5% to fund 
‘Six Networks’ infrastructure projects and raised its relending 
quotas. A new interest subsidy on first-home mortgages 
provides limited support to demand. We expect no policy rate 
cut this year. There is still room to speed up fiscal spending, 
including by using CNY 500bn of unused local-government 
debt quota. If the PBoC switches to an overnight reverse repo 
rate as its policy rate, that would imply a de facto rate cut as 
the overnight rate is lower than the 7-day official policy rate. 
BoJ to hike at a quarterly pace until Q2 2027. Japan's 
economy remains solid. Wages are growing above 4%, and 
the labour market is tight. The main headwind is high energy 
prices, which is slowing growth. Fiscal spending gives some 
support: the FY27 draft budget includes ambitious spending 
plans, alongside a proposed two-year cut to the consumption 
tax on food and a new five-year defence programme. 
However, this extra spending raises questions about fiscal 
sustainability. The BoJ unveiled plans to triple the pace of ETF 
sales from January 2027, potentially funding the tax cut. 
Price pressures now look firmly established. Headline and 
core inflation rose above 2% for the first time this year, and 
core-core inflation (ex-fresh food and energy) jumped to 2.4% 
and is expected to rise towards 3% in Q1 2027. Breakeven 
inflation rates have stayed above 2% most of this year. Real 
interest rates remain negative, hurting savers and pensioners. 
The BoJ held its policy rate at 1.25% in October, after raising 
it in September, only three months after its previous hike. This 
points to an accelerated quarterly hiking pace. We expect the 
quarterly pace of hikes to continue, taking the rate to 2% by 
June 2027. Money markets have caught up with our hawkish 
view in recent months. The BoJ noted for the first time that 
underlying inflation has reached its 2% target, marking a shift 
from creating inflation to containing it. Politics could slow the 
hiking cycle in H2 2027. Two government-appointed board 
members voted against the September hike, and the two most 
hawkish members are due to leave in July 2027.

### Page 9

Global Market Outlook 9

Our view

Foundation: Bond yields remain near multi-year highs, offering an attractive entry point. We view fixed income as a Core holding 
(ie, a Neutral allocation) in our multi-asset portfolios. Within the asset class, we maintain an Overweight stance on EM USD 
government bonds, given their attractive risk-reward. We hold an Underweight view on Developed Market (DM) government 
bonds due to the tail risks associated with term premia. 
The Fed’s decision to hold rates at 3.75-4.00% in October amid still-elevated energy prices suggests that every Fed meeting 
from here is ‘live’, with a rate change possible on incoming data. With US inflation still running hot and the labour market tight, 
we expect two more 25bps hikes (one by year-end and one in H1 2027), bringing the Fed’s target range for the federal funds 
rate to 4.25-4.5%. We continue to favour the 3-7-year part of the US government bond yield curve as the best hedge against 
any risk-off move driven by AI-related growth fears. As inflation pressures ease in H2 2027, we see room for the Fed to consider 
a rate cut, which should pull nominal yields lower. Solid corporate fundamentals and supportive technicals should keep spreads 
tight, but there is limited room for them to tighten further. We still favour EM USD over local-currency (LCY) government bonds. 
Opportunistic ideas: We are bullish on US TIPS, AAA-rated CLOs, broad US HYs and Australian dollar (AUD) corporate bonds.

DM rates – Underweight 
We remain Underweight DM government bonds. In the US, 
government bond yields have retraced meaningfully after a 
sharp bear steepening through September and early October. 
We expect real yields to stay elevated over the next six 
months as AI capex and productivity growth continue to drive 
above-trend economic growth. We expect two more 25bps 
rate hikes by June 2027.  
Over the next few months, we expect US government bond 
yields to remain rangebound. The near-term path hinges on 
five factors: 1) the 3 November US mid-term election, where 
a ‘Democratic sweep’ would likely be bond-supportive by 
constraining US President Trump’s fiscal spending agenda, 
while a ‘Republican sweep’ would likely weigh on bonds as 
markets price in further fiscal expansion and a higher term 
premium; 2) Fed communication at upcoming policy meetings; 
3) the US Treasury’s quarterly refunding announcement on 4 
November, where any signal of reduced long-end issuance 
would likely compress long-end yields; 4) softening auction 
demand and repatriation by foreign investors, which add 
uncertainty around demand for US government bonds; and 5) 
the fluid geopolitical situation in the Middle East, which is 
keeping oil prices elevated, with a risk of further upside. 
Going into H2 2027, we expect moderating oil prices and the 
fading inflationary impact of tariffs to give the Fed room to 
consider rate cuts. The US 30-year yield, however, is likely to 
stay elevated as fiscal deficit remains substantial next year.  
We expect a final ECB hike to 2.75% by December 2026, then 
a pause in H1 2027, followed by a 25bps cut in Q3 2027. In 
Japan, we expect quarterly pace of BoJ hikes, with one hike 
that takes the policy rate to 1.50% by December 2026 and two 
more in H1 2027, reaching 2.00% before pausing at mid-year.
